DEFINE a2k_base_year         = 50 #                                     0000001400000200  
% Base year value for this system                                       0000001500000250  
                                                                        0000001600000300  
      ,a2k_century_19        = 19 #                                     0000001700000400  
% Century value for this century                                        0000001800000450  
                                                                        0000001900000500  
      ,a2k_century_20        = 20 #                                     0000002000000600  
% Century value for next century                                        0000002100000650  
                                                                        0000002200000700  
      ,a2k_amd_wt_aa         = 10000 #                                  0000002300000800  
      ,a2k_camd_wt_cc        = 1000000 #                                0000002400001000  
      ;                                                                 0000002500001200  
                                                                        0000002600001400  
INTEGER a2k_date_int_8                                                  0000002700001450  
% Temporary integer date variable for date manipulation                 0000002800001460  
       ;                                                                0000002900001500  
                                                                        0000003000001550  
EBCDIC ARRAY a2k_date_ebcdic_8 [0:7]                                    0000003100001600  
% Temporary ebcdic array of size 8 for date manipulation                0000003200001620  
            ,a2k_date_ebcdic_5 [0:4]                                    0000003300001622  
% Temporary ebcdic array of size 5 for julian date manipulation         0000003400001624  
            ,a2k_date_ebcdic_7 [0:6]                                    0000003500001630  
% Temporary ebcdic array of size 7 for julian date manipulation         0000003600001640  
            ;                                                           0000003700001650

PROCEDURE a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_int (a2k_date_int_6                      0000004500002000  
                                   ,a2k_date_int_8                      0000004600004000  
                                   );                                   0000004700006000  
                                                                        0000004800008000  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_int                   0000004900010000  
%                                                                       0000005000014000  
% Input Parameter     :      Six digit date in AAMMDD format in an      0000005100016000  
%                            integer variable                           0000005200018000  
% Output parameter    :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000005300020000  
%                            intager variable                           0000005400022000  
%                                                                       0000005500024000  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the six digit date  0000005600024200  
%                     :      AAMMDD format into an integer variable. It 0000005700024400  
%                     :      calculates the century value based on base 0000005800024600  
%                     :      year concept and returns the date in       0000005900024800  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format in another integer variable0000006000025000  
                                                                        0000006100025200  
INTEGER a2k_date_int_6                                                  0000006200026000  
       ,a2k_date_int_8                                                  0000006300028000  
       ;                                                                0000006400030000  
                                                                        0000006500032000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000006600034000  
                                                                        0000006700036000  
    INTEGER a2k_year                                                    0000006800048000  
           ;                                                            0000006900050000  
                                                                        0000007000060000  
    % Compare the year field with the base year value                   0000007100062000  
    a2k_year := a2k_date_int_6 - a2k_base_year * a2k_amd_wt_aa ;        0000007200064000  
                                                                        0000007300074000  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000007400076000  
    IF a2k_year geq 0 THEN                                              0000007500078000  
        a2k_date_int_8 := a2k_date_int_6 + a2k_century_19               0000007600080000  
                          * a2k_camd_wt_cc                              0000007700080500  
    ELSE                                                                0000007800082000  
        a2k_date_int_8 := a2k_date_int_6 + a2k_century_20 *             0000007900084000  
                          a2k_camd_wt_cc ;                              0000008000090000  
    END;                                                                0000008100092000  
                                                                        0000008200092050  
PROCEDURE a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_ebcdic (a2k_date_six                     0000008300092100  
                                      ,a2k_date_eight                   0000008400092200  
                                      );                                0000008500092300  
                                                                        0000008600092400  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_ebcdic                0000008700092500  
%                                                                       0000008800092600  
% Input Parameter     :      Six digit date in AAMMDD format in an      0000008900092700  
%                            ebcdic array                               0000009000092800  
% Output parameter    :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000009100092900  
%                            ebcdic array                               0000009200093000  
%                                                                       0000009300093100  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the six digit date  0000009400093200  
%                     :      AAMMDD format into an ebcdic array. It     0000009500093300  
%                     :      calculates the century value based on base 0000009600093400  
%                     :      year concept and returns the date in       0000009700093500  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format in another ebcdic array of 0000009800093600  
%                     :      size 8 bytes.                              0000009900093700  
                                                                        0000010000093800  
                                                                        0000010100093900  
EBCDIC ARRAY a2k_date_six [0]                                           0000010200094000  
            ,a2k_date_eight [0]                                         0000010300094100  
            ;                                                           0000010400094200  
                                                                        0000010500094300  
    BEGIN                                                               0000010600094400  
                                                                        0000010700094500  
    INTEGER year                                                        0000010800095100  
           ;                                                            0000010900095200  
                                                                        0000011000095300  
    POINTER a2k_date_six_ptr                                            0000011100095400  
           ,a2k_date_eight_ptr                                          0000011200095500  
           ;                                                            0000011300095600  
                                                                        0000011400095700  
    % Separating out the year part from the six digit date field        0000011500095800  
    year := INTEGER (a2k_date_six[0],2);                                0000011600095900  
                                                                        0000011700096000  
    % Initialising pointers                                             0000011800096100  
    a2k_date_six_ptr := a2k_date_six [0] ;                              0000011900096200  
    a2k_date_eight_ptr := a2k_date_eight [0] ;                          0000012000096300  
                                                                        0000012100096400  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000012200096500  
    IF year geq a2k_base_year THEN                                      0000012300096600  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000012400096700  
            STRING(a2k_century_19,*)                                    0000012500096750  
    ELSE                                                                0000012600096800  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000012700096900  
            STRING(a2k_century_20,*);                                   0000012800096950  
                                                                        0000012900097000  
    REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr BY a2k_date_six_ptr for                  0000013000097100  
        size(a2k_date_six);                                             0000013100097150  
                                                                        0000013200097200  
    END;                                                                0000013300097300  
                                                                        0000013400098000  
PROCEDURE a2k_conv_camd_to_amd_int (a2k_date_int_8                      0000013500100000  
                                   ,a2k_date_int_6                      0000013600102000  
                                   );                                   0000013700104000  
                                                                        0000013800106000  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_camd_to_amd_int                   0000013900108000  
%                                                                       0000014000110000  
% Input Parameter     :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000014100112000  
%                            integer variable                           0000014200114000  
% Output parameter    :      Six digit date in AAMMDD format in an      0000014300116000  
%                            intager variable                           0000014400118000  
%                                                                       0000014500120000  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the eight digit date0000014600122000  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format into an integer variable.It0000014700124000  
%                     :      truncates the century value and returns the0000014800126000  
%                     :      date in AAMMDD format in another integer   0000014900128000  
%                     :      variable.                                  0000015000130000  
                                                                        0000015100132000  
INTEGER a2k_date_int_8                                                  0000015200134000  
       ,a2k_date_int_6                                                  0000015300136000  
       ;                                                                0000015400138000  
                                                                        0000015500140000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000015600142000  
                                                                        0000015700144000  
    % Truncating the century field from 8 digit date to make it a six   0000015800146000  
    % digit date                                                        0000015900148000  
    a2k_date_int_6 := a2k_date_int_8 MOD a2k_camd_wt_cc;                0000016000150000  
                                                                        0000016100152000  
    END;                                                                0000016200154000  
                                                                        0000016300156000  
PROCEDURE a2k_conv_ad_to_cad_ebcdic (a2k_date_five                      0000016400158000  
                                    ,a2k_date_seven                     0000016500160000  
                                    );                                  0000016600162000  
                                                                        0000016700164000  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_ad_to_cad_ebcdic                  0000016800166000  
%                                                                       0000016900168000  
% Input Parameter     :      Five digit date in AADDD julian format in  0000017000170000  
%                            an ebcdic array                            0000017100172000  
% Output parameter    :      Seven digit date in CCAADDD format in an   0000017200174000  
%                            ebcdic array                               0000017300176000  
%                                                                       0000017400178000  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the five digit date 0000017500180000  
%                     :      AADDD julian format into an ebcdic array.It0000017600182000  
%                     :      calculates the century value based on base 0000017700184000  
%                     :      year concept and returns the date in julian0000017800186000  
%                     :      CCAADDD format in another ebcdic array of  0000017900188000  
%                     :      size 7 bytes.                              0000018000190000  
                                                                        0000018100192000  
                                                                        0000018200194000  
EBCDIC ARRAY a2k_date_five [0]                                          0000018300196000  
            ,a2k_date_seven [0]                                         0000018400198000  
            ;                                                           0000018500200000  
                                                                        0000018600202000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000018700204000  
                                                                        0000018800206000  
    INTEGER year                                                        0000018900208000  
           ;                                                            0000019000210000  
                                                                        0000019100212000  
    POINTER a2k_date_five_ptr                                           0000019200214000  
           ,a2k_date_seven_ptr                                          0000019300216000  
           ;                                                            0000019400218000  
                                                                        0000019500220000  
    % Separating out the year part from the five digit date field       0000019600222000  
    year := INTEGER (a2k_date_five[0],2);                               0000019700224000  
                                                                        0000019800226000  
    % Initialising pointers                                             0000019900228000  
    a2k_date_five_ptr := a2k_date_five [0] ;                            0000020000230000  
    a2k_date_seven_ptr := a2k_date_seven [0] ;                          0000020100232000  
                                                                        0000020200234000  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000020300236000  
    IF year geq a2k_base_year THEN                                      0000020400238000  
        REPLACE a2k_date_seven_ptr:a2k_date_seven_ptr BY                0000020500240000  
            STRING(a2k_century_19,*)                                    0000020600242000  
    ELSE                                                                0000020700244000  
        REPLACE a2k_date_seven_ptr:a2k_date_seven_ptr BY                0000020800246000  
            STRING(a2k_century_20,*);                                   0000020900248000  
                                                                        0000021000250000  
    REPLACE a2k_date_seven_ptr BY a2k_date_five_ptr for                 0000021100252000  
        size(a2k_date_five);                                            0000021200254000  
                                                                        0000021300256000  
    END;                                                                0000021400258000

PROCEDURE a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_hex (a2k_date_six                        0000021700258300  
                                   ,a2k_date_eight                      0000021800258400  
                                   );                                   0000021900258500  
                                                                        0000022000258600  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_hex                   0000022100258700

% Output parameter    :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000022500259100  
%                            hex array                                  0000022600259200  
%                                                                       0000022700259300  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the six digit date  0000022800259400  
%                     :      AAMMDD format into an hex array.           0000022900259500  
%                     :      It calculates the century value based on   0000023000259600  
%                     :      base year concept and returns the date in  0000023100259700  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format in another hex array .     0000023200259800  
                                                                        0000023300260000  
                                                                        0000023400260100  
HEX ARRAY a2k_date_six [0]                                              0000023500260200  
         ,a2k_date_eight [0]                                            0000023600260300  
         ;                                                              0000023700260400  
                                                                        0000023800260500  
    BEGIN                                                               0000023900260600  
                                                                        0000024000260700  
    INTEGER year                                                        0000024100260800  
           ;                                                            0000024200260900  
                                                                        0000024300261000  
    POINTER a2k_date_six_ptr                                            0000024400261100  
           ,a2k_date_eight_ptr                                          0000024500261200  
           ;                                                            0000024600261300  
                                                                        0000024700261400  
    % Separating out the year part from the six digit date field        0000024800261500  
    year := INTEGER (a2k_date_six[0],2);                                0000024900261600  
                                                                        0000025000261700  
    % Initialising pointers                                             0000025100261800  
    a2k_date_six_ptr := a2k_date_six [0] ;                              0000025200261900  
    a2k_date_eight_ptr := a2k_date_eight [0] ;                          0000025300262000  
                                                                        0000025400262100  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000025500262200  
    IF year geq a2k_base_year THEN                                      0000025600262300  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000025700262400  
            a2k_century_19 FOR 2 DIGITS                                 0000025800262500  
    ELSE                                                                0000025900262600  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000026000262700  
            a2k_century_20 FOR 2 DIGITS;                                0000026100262800  
                                                                        0000026200262900  
    REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr BY a2k_date_six_ptr for                  0000026300263000  
        size(a2k_date_six);                                             0000026400263100  
                                                                        0000026500263200  
    END;                                                                0000026600263300

%    SISDFECARM(A,N)=        A[255+ 24*N]       #, % 06                 0000092300644011  
%   ISISDNIVARM(A,N)=INTEGER(A[261+ 24*N] ,08)  #, % 08                 0000092400645011  
%    SISDNIVARM(A,N)=        A[261+ 24*N]       #, % 08                 0000092500646011  
%   ISISDNIVBDM(A,N)=INTEGER(A[269+ 24*N] ,08)  #, % 08                 0000092600647011  
%    SISDNIVBDM(A,N)=        A[269+ 24*N]       #, % 08                 0000092700648011  
%   ISISDSTAARM(A,N)=INTEGER(A[277+ 24*N] ,02)  #, % 02                 0000092800649011  
%    SISDSTAARM(A,N)=        A[277+ 24*N]       #, % 02                 0000092900650011  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000093000651011  
    ISISDFECPRO(A) = INTEGER(A[08]      , 08)   #,                      0000093100652011  
     SISDFECPRO(A) =         A[08]              #, % 08                 0000093200653011  
    ISISDFECCON(A) = INTEGER(A[16]      , 08)   #,                      0000093300654011  
     SISDFECCON(A) =         A[16]              #, % 08                 0000093400655011  
    ISISDFEC151(A) = INTEGER(A[24]      , 08)   #,                      0000093500656011  
     SISDFEC151(A) =         A[24]              #, % 08                 0000093600657011  
    ISISDSTAREG(A) = INTEGER(A[32]      , 02)   #,                      0000093700658011  
     SISDSTAREG(A) =         A[32]              #, % 02                 0000093800659011  
     SISDNOMSDO(A) =         A[34]              #, % 34                 0000093900660011  
     SISDPKSDOS(A) =         A[68]              #, % 17                 0000094000661011  
     SISDNOMMOV(A) =         A[85]              #, % 34                 0000094100662011  
     SISDPKMOVS(A) =         A[119]             #, % 17                 0000094200663011  
     SISDNOMDES(A) =         A[136]             #, % 34                 0000094300664011  
     SISDPKDESS(A) =         A[170]             #, % 17                 0000094400665011  
     SISDNOMERR(A) =         A[187]             #, % 34                 0000094500666011  
     SISDPKERRS(A) =         A[221]             #, % 17                 0000094600667011  
     SISDNOMBDM(A) =         A[238]             #, % 17                 0000094700668011  
     SISDSTABDM(A) =         A[255]             #, % 02                 0000094800669011  
     SISDNUMARC(A) =         A[257]             #, % 02                 0000094900670011  
     SISDTERNIV(A) =         A[259]             #, % 02                 0000095000671011  
    ISISDFECARM(A,N)=INTEGER(A[261+ 26*N] ,08)  #, % 06                 0000095100672011  
     SISDFECARM(A,N)=        A[261+ 26*N]       #, % 06                 0000095200673011  
    ISISDNIVARM(A,N)=INTEGER(A[269+ 26*N] ,08)  #, % 08                 0000095300674011  
     SISDNIVARM(A,N)=        A[269+ 26*N]       #, % 08                 0000095400675011  
    ISISDNIVBDM(A,N)=INTEGER(A[277+ 26*N] ,08)  #, % 08                 0000095500676011  
     SISDNIVBDM(A,N)=        A[277+ 26*N]       #, % 08                 0000095600677011  
    ISISDSTAARM(A,N)=INTEGER(A[285+ 26*N] ,02)  #, % 02                 0000095700678011  
     SISDSTAARM(A,N)=        A[285+ 26*N]       #, % 02                 0000095800679011  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000095900680011  
                                                                        0000096000681011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000096100682011  
    SNR = MYSELF.MIXNUMBER#,                                            0000096200683011  
                                                                        0000096300684011  
    ACQUIREL(E,A) = BEGIN                                               0000096400685011  
                      IF READLOCK(SNR,A) NEQ 0 THEN                     0000096500686011  
                         DO PROCURE(E) UNTIL READLOCK(-1,A) = 0;        0000096600687011  
                    END#,                                               0000096700688011  
    RELINQUISHL(E,A) = BEGIN                                            0000096800689011  
                         IF READLOCK(0,A) NEQ SNR THEN                  0000096900690011  
                            LIBERATE(E);                                0000097000691011  
                       END#,                                            0000097100692011  
                                                                        0000097200693011  
                                                                        0000097300694011  
    TIPOMSG = MSGLJ[14]#,                                               0000097400695011  
    CODMSG  = MSGLJ[16]#,                                               0000097500696011  
    TXTMSG  = MSGLJ[23]#,                                               0000097600697011  
                                                                        0000097700698011  
    HDRMSG  = "S151/L011/001:"#,                                        0000097800699011  
    CLRMSG  = REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)#,                    0000097900700011  
    ERRORDB = BEGIN                                                     0000098000701011  
                 REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                  0000098100702011  
                 REPLACE MSGLJ     BY HDRMSG, "E>04",                   0000098200703011  
                         DMERR.DMERROR      FOR 2 DIGITS,               0000098300704011  
                         DMERR.DMERRORTYPE  FOR 2 DIGITS,               0000098400705011  
                         "< LIBRARY CONSMOVS,  ERROR EN DMSII ",        0000098500706011  
                         "ESTRUCTUR ",DMERR.DMSTRUCTURE FOR 2 DIGITS;   0000098600707011  
                 LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                       0000098700708011  
                 ERROR:=9;                                              0000098800709011  
              END#;                                                     0000098900710011

 PROCEDURE CARGAFECHAS;                                                 0000100200723011  
           FORWARD;                                                     0000100300724011  
%****************************************************************       0000100400725011  
                                                                        0000100500726011  
  EBCDIC ARRAY LIBNAME[0:50],MSGLJ[0:273],NOM_BDM[0:45],                0000100600727011  
%              ACONTROLE1,ACONTROLE2[0:530],PARAM[0:255] ,              0000100700728011  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000100800729011  
               ACONTROLE1,ACONTROLE2[0:560],PARAM[0:255] ,              0000100900730011  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000101000731011  
               ARC_MOV,ARC_DES[0:34],PK_MOV,PK_DES[0:17],               0000101100732011  
               NOM_ARC[0:70];                                           0000101200733011  
  ARRAY  ACLAVES[0:50000],NOM_LIB[0:59], ARREGLO[0:74],LEYENTXC[0:10],  0000101300734011  
         LEYENTXC2[0:10],LEYENTXC3[0:10],LEYENTXC4[0:10],               0000101400734211  
         LEYENTXC5[0:10],AMEMTAXCOM[0:99990],AMEMTAXCOM2[0:39996];      0000101500734500  
  REAL    DMERR;                                                        0000101600735011  
  BOOLEAN BASE_OK,MONITOR;                                              0000101700736011  
  INTEGER CONLJ,ERROR,SIST_LIB,CSI_LIB,FECPROC,CUAL_EVENTO,IND, RESULT, 0000101800737011  
          A1, A2, A3, A4, A5, A6, A7, A8, A9, FORMATO_CAT;              0000101900738011  
  INTEGER ARRAY FEC_DIA[0:9];                                           0000102000739011  
  STRING  BD_NAME;                                                      0000102100740011  
  EVENT   E_ANT,BASE_OPEN, E1, E2, E3, E4, E5, E6, E7, E8, E9, E10,     0000102200741011  
          CONXCAJA, CON3NIVEL, CONXCNT, CON4ULT, CONXSUC, CON_PLATINO,  0000102300742011  
          CONXSUCMDE, CONSULTACI, CON_MESP;                             0000102400743011

%=================================================================      0000102700746011  
% RUTINA DE CONTROL DE LA LIBRERIA                                      0000102800747011  
%================================================================       0000102900748011  
 PROCEDURE PROCTL;                                                      0000103000749011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000103100750011  
   PROCEDURE EJECUTA_HI;                                                0000103200751011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000103300752011  
     IF HAPPENED(BASE_OPEN) THEN   CAUSE(E_ANT)                         0000103400753011  
     ELSE   RESET(E_ANT);                                               0000103500754011  
     IF MYSELF.TASKVALUE = 1 OR MYSELF.TASKVALUE = 3 THEN               0000103600755011  
     BEGIN                                                              0000103700756011  
        RESET(BASE_OPEN);                                               0000103800757011  
        WAIT(CONXCAJA);                                                 0000103900758011  
        WAIT(CON3NIVEL);                                                0000104000759011  
        WAIT(CON_MESP);                                                 0000104100760011  
        WAIT(CONXCNT);                                                  0000104200761011  
        WAIT(CON_PLATINO);                                              0000104300761021  
        WAIT(CON4ULT);                                                  0000104400762011  
        WAIT(CONXSUC);                                                  0000104500763011  
        WAIT(CONXSUCMDE);                                               0000104600764011  
        WAIT(CONSULTACI);                                               0000104700765011  
     END;                                                               0000104800766011  
     CASE MYSELF.TASKVALUE OF                                           0000104900767011  
     BEGIN                                                              0000105000768011  
        1 : IF BASE_OK THEN                                             0000105100769011  
            BEGIN                                                       0000105200770011  
               CLOSE S151BD10MOVDIA151 :DMERR;                          0000105300771011  
               IF NOT BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                               0000105400772011  
               BEGIN                                                    0000105500773011  
                  IND := -1;                                            0000105600774011  
                  WHILE IND:=* + 1 LEQ 9 DO                             0000105700775011  
                  BEGIN                                                 0000105800776011  
                    CLOSE(MOVS[IND]);                                   0000105900777011  
                    CLOSE(DESS[IND]);                                   0000106000778011  
                  END;                                                  0000106100779011  
                  BASE_OK := FALSE;                                     0000106200780011  
                  RESET(BASE_OPEN);                                     0000106300781011  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG "I>050110<LIBRARY L011/",     0000106400782011  
                               "CONSMOVS BLOQUEA ACCESO A B. D. ",      0000106500783011  
                               NOM_BDM FOR 23;                          0000106600784011  
%                LJ(CONLJ, MSGLJ);                                      0000106700785011  
               END                                                      0000106800786011  
               ELSE                                                     0000106900787011  
                  ERRORDB;                                              0000107000788011  
            END                                                         0000107100789011  
            ELSE                                                        0000107200790011  
            BEGIN                                                       0000107300791011  
               IF HAPPENED(E_ANT) THEN  % LA BASE ESTA CERRADA          0000107400792011  
                  CAUSE(BASE_OPEN);                                     0000107500793011  
%                                      NOM_BDM FOR 23;                  0000107600794011  
%              LJ(CONLJ, MSGLJ);                                        0000107700795011  
            END;                                                        0000107800796011  
        5 : MONITOR := TRUE;                                            0000107900796111  
        6 : MONITOR := FALSE;                                           0000108000796211  
        3 : IF NOT BASE_OK THEN                                         0000108100797011  
            BEGIN                                                       0000108200798011  
               CARGAFECHAS;                                             0000108300799011  
               S151BD10MOVDIA151.TITLE := BD_NAME;                      0000108400800011  
               OPEN INQUIRY S151BD10MOVDIA151:DMERR;                    0000108500801011  
               IF NOT BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                               0000108600802011  
               BEGIN                                                    0000108700803011  
                  CAUSE(BASE_OPEN);                                     0000108800804011  
                  BASE_OK := TRUE;                                      0000108900805011  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG "I>050110<LIBRARY L011",      0000109000806011  
                               "/CONSMOVS RESTAURA ACCESO A B. D. ",    0000109100807011  
                               NOM_BDM FOR 23;                          0000109200808011  
%                 LJ(CONLJ, MSGLJ);                                     0000109300809011  
               END                                                      0000109400810011  
                 ERRORDB;                                               0000109500811011  
            END                                                         0000109600812011  
            ELSE                                                        0000109700813011  
            BEGIN                                                       0000109800814011  
               IF HAPPENED(E_ANT) THEN  % LA BASE ESTA ABIERTA          0000109900815011  
                  CAUSE(BASE_OPEN);     %                               0000110000816011  
%                                      NOM_BDM FOR 23;                  0000110100817011  
%              LJ(CONLJ, MSGLJ);                                        0000110200818011  
            END;                                                        0000110300819011  
        ELSE : BEGIN                                                    0000110400820011  
                  IF MYSELF.TASKVALUE > 0 THEN                          0000110500821011  
                  BEGIN                                                 0000110600822011  
                    DISPLAY (" HI 1 CIERRA BASE DE DATOS ");            0000110700823011  
                    DISPLAY (" HI 3 ABRE BASE DE DATOS ");              0000110800824011  
                    DISPLAY (" HI 5 ENCIENDE MONITOR     ");            0000110900824111  
                    DISPLAY (" HI 6 APAGA MONITOR      ");              0000111000824211  
                  END;                                                  0000111100825011  
                  IF HAPPENED(E_ANT) THEN                               0000111200826011  
                     CAUSE(BASE_OPEN);                                  0000111300827011  
               END;                                                     0000111400828011  
        END;                                                            0000111500829011  
    RESET(MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);                                       0000111600830011  
    MYSELF.TASKVALUE := 0;                                              0000111700831011  
  END;                                                                  0000111800832011  
%=================================================================      0000111900833011  
%OUTER  PROCEDURE PROCTL                                        =       0000112000834011  
%================================================================       0000112100835011  
  WHILE MYSELF.LIBRARYUSERS > 0 DO                                      0000112200836011  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000112300837011  
     CUAL_EVENTO := WAITANDRESET((2), MYSELF.EXCEPTIONEVENT);           0000112400838011  
     CASE CUAL_EVENTO OF                                                0000112500839011  
     BEGIN                                                              0000112600840011  
        1 : CUAL_EVENTO := 0;                                           0000112700841011  
        2 : EJECUTA_HI ;                                                0000112800842011  
        ELSE :                 ;                                        0000112900843011  
     END;                                                               0000113000844011  
  END;                                                                  0000113100845011  
  MYSELF.STATUS := VALUE(GOINGAWAY);                                    0000113200846011  
 END;                                                                   0000113300847011

  INTEGER PROCEDURE BDACCESS(DATOS);                                    0000113600850011  
  EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                0000113700851011  
% *****************************************************************     0000113800852011  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000113900853011  
                                                                        0000114000854011  
    EPILOG PROCEDURE LIBERA;                                            0000114100855011  
      RELINQUISHL(E1, A1);                                              0000114200856011  
                                                                        0000114300857011  
    DEFINE FUNCION = INTEGER(DATOS[0],2) #;                             0000114400858011  
    ACQUIREL(E1,A1);                                                    0000114500859011  
    IF FUNCION = 98 THEN                                                0000114600860011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000114700861011  
       IF BASE_OK THEN                                                  0000114800862011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000114900863011  
          BASE_OK   := FALSE;                                           0000115000864011  
          RESET(BASE_OPEN);                                             0000115100865011  
          CLOSE S151BD10MOVDIA151;                                      0000115200866011  
          CLRMSG;                                                       0000115300867011  
          REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"I>050110< LIBRARY L011/CONSMOVS,",   0000115400868011  
                  " BLOQUEA ACCESO A B.D. ",                            0000115500869011  
                  NOM_BDM FOR 23;                                       0000115600870011  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000115700871011  
       END                                                              0000115800872011  
    END                                                                 0000115900873011  
    ELSE                                                                0000116000874011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000116100875011  
          IF NOT BASE_OK THEN                                           0000116200876011  
          BEGIN                                                         0000116300877011  
             CARGAFECHAS;                                               0000116400878011  
             OPEN INQUIRY S151BD10MOVDIA151:DMERR;                      0000116500879011  
             IF NOT BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                 0000116600880011  
             BEGIN                                                      0000116700881011  
                CAUSE(BASE_OPEN);                                       0000116800882011  
                BASE_OK   := TRUE;                                      0000116900883011  
                CLRMSG;                                                 0000117000884011  
                REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"I>050110< LIBRARY L011/",      0000117100885011  
                      "CONSMOVS, RESTAURA ACCESO A B.D. ",              0000117200886011  
                      NOM_BDM FOR 23;                                   0000117300887011  
                LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                        0000117400888011  
             END                                                        0000117500889011  
          END;                                                          0000117600890011  
       END;                                                             0000117700891011  
  END OF BDACCESS;                                                      0000117800892011

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000152001218031  
%% PROCEDIMIENTO PARA LEER DATOS ADICIONALES Y RECUPERAR              % 0000152101218051  
%% LAS LEYENDAS                                                       % 0000152201218071  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000152301218091  
 PROCEDURE OBTIENE_LEYENDAS;                                            0000152401218111  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000152501218131  
    INTEGER M;                                                          0000152601218151  
                                                                        0000152701218161  
    IF NIVDES1 > 0 THEN                                                 0000152801218171  
    BEGIN                                                               0000152901218191  
       M := NUM_FILE + 10;                                              0000153001218211  
       REPLACE CONTROLREG[0] BY M FOR 4;                                0000153101218231  
       CONTROLES(CONTROLREG);                                           0000153201218251  
                                                                        0000153301218261  
       IF READ(DESS[NUM_FILE][NIVDES1],90,ADES) THEN                    0000153401218271  
          REPLACE LEYENDA(0) BY " " FOR 200                             0000153501218291  
       ELSE                                                             0000153601218311  
       BEGIN                                                            0000153701218316  
          REPLACE LEYENDA(0) BY " " FOR 200            ;                0000153801218321  
          REPLACE LEYENDA(0) BY DFLEYENDA1(ADES) FOR 400;               0000153901218331  
       END                                                              0000154001218341  
    END                                                                 0000154101218351  
    ELSE                                                                0000154201218371  
       REPLACE LEYENDA(0) BY " " FOR 200            ;                   0000154301218391  
 END OF OBTIENE_LEYENDA;                                                0000154401218411  
                                                                        0000154501219011  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000154601220011  
 PROCEDURE REPLACE_DATOS;                                               0000154701221011  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000154801222011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000154901223011  
    IMPORTE := IMPORTE*100;                                             0000155001224011  
    IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                             0000155101225011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000155201226011  
         CLAVTRAN := STRING(IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND),4);                   0000155301227011  
         TIPOMOVI := STRING(IDFESQOCUR(ALOG,GIND),1);                   0000155401228011  
         CLAVTIPOMOVI := CLAVTRAN !! TIPOMOVI;                          0000155501229011  
         MOVCVETIPO:= DECIMAL(CLAVTIPOMOVI);                            0000155601230011  
         CVETRA := MOVCVETIPO;                                          0000155701231011  
         ENVIOEDC:= IADFINDEDC(CVETRA);                                 0000155801232011  
       END                                                              0000155901233011  
    ELSE                                                                0000156001234011  
    ENVIOEDC:= IADFINDEDC(CVETRA);                                      0000156101235011  
    IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                             0000156201237011  
       IF INDI LEQ NMOV AND ENVIOEDC NEQ 5 THEN                         0000156301238011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000156401239011  
          REPLACE DCVEMDE(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000156501240011  
                                   SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000156601241011  
                                   CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000156701242011  
                                   AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000156801243011  
                                   CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000156901244011  
                                   REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,           0000157001245011  
                                  (IMPORTE DIV 100000000)               0000157101246011  
                                           MOD 100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS,  0000157201247011  
                                   IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000157301248011  
                                   HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,           0000157401249011  
                                   ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS;           0000157501250011  
          REPLACE DREFCTE(INDI) BY MREFCECOBAN  FOR 7 DIGITS;           0000157601251011  
          REPLACE LREFCTE(INDI) BY LEYENDA(0) FOR  80;                  0000157701251211  
          REPLACE DAUTS151      BY AUTS151    FOR   8 DIGITS;           0000157801252011  
          INDI:= * + 1;                                                 0000157901253011  
       END                                                              0000158001254011  
       ELSE                                                             0000158101255011  
                                                                        0000158201256011  
    ELSE                                                                0000158301257011  
    IF INDI LEQ NMOV AND (ENVIOEDC NEQ 5 OR CVETRA=1009 OR              0000158401258011  
                                            CVETRA=1072) THEN           0000158501259011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000158601260011  
       REPLACE DCVEMDE(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,              0000158701261011  
                                SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,              0000158801262011  
                                CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,              0000158901263011  
                                AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,              0000159001264011  
                                CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,              0000159101265011  
                                REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,              0000159201266011  
                               (IMPORTE DIV 100000000)                  0000159301267011  
                                        MOD 100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS,     0000159401268011  
                                IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,              0000159501269011  
                                HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,              0000159601270011  
                                ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS;              0000159701271011  
       REPLACE DREFCTE(INDI) BY MREFCECOBAN FOR  7 DIGITS;              0000159801272011  
       REPLACE LREFCTE(INDI) BY LEYENDA(0) FOR  80;                     0000159901272211  
       REPLACE DAUTS151      BY AUTS151    FOR   8 DIGITS;              0000160001273011  
       INDI:= * + 1;                                                    0000160101274011  
    END;                                                                0000160201275011  
 END OF REPLACE_DATOS;                                                  0000160301276011  
                                                                        0000160401277011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000160501278011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_IMP_ADIC;                                       0000160601279011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000160701280011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000160801281011  
    INTEGER RSLT_NEXT,IND;                                              0000160901282011  
    FIND_IMP_ADIC:= 0;                                                  0000161001283011  
                                                                        0000161101284011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000161201285011  
       RSLT_NEXT := 7                                                   0000161301286011  
    ELSE                                                                0000161401287011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000161501288011  
    CASE NUMDIA OF                                                      0000161601289011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000161701290011  
         0:BEGIN                                                        0000161801291011  
              FIND B02SXIMPADI AT B02-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151:DMERR;      0000161901292011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000162001293011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000162101294011  
              ELSE                                                      0000162201295011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000162301296011

%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000222301465321  
 PROCEDURE REPLACE_DATOS;                                               0000222401465331  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000222501465341  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000222601465351  
    POINTER PTR_EBCDIC ;                                                0000222701465361  
    IF ESTATUS = 6 THEN                                                 0000222801465371  
    ELSE                                                                0000222901465381  
       IMPORTE := IMPORTE*100;                                          0000223001465391  
    IF NUM LEQ 5 THEN                                                   0000223101465401  
       REPLACE DINDMOVS    BY   NUM FOR 2 DIGITS                        0000223201465411  
    ELSE                                                                0000223301465421  
       REPLACE DINDMOVS    BY   0   FOR 2 DIGITS;                       0000223401465423  
    IF SISTEMA = 500 AND                                                0000223501465425  
      (CVETRA = 1134 OR                                                 0000223601465427  
       CVETRA = 1135 OR                                                 0000223701465428  
       CVETRA = 1136 OR                                                 0000223801465429  
       CVETRA = 1137 OR                                                 0000223901465430  
       CVETRA = 2227 OR                                                 0000224001465431  
       CVETRA = 2228) THEN                                              0000224101465432  
       REFER:= MREFCECOBAN                                              0000224201465434  
    ELSE                                                                0000224301465437  
       IF SISTEMA = 500    AND                                          0000224401465438  
         (CVETRA   = 1133  OR                                           0000224501465439  
          CVETRA   = 2226) THEN                                         0000224601465440  
          REFER:= DOUBLE((POINTER(ADES)+58), 10);                       0000224701465442  
    IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                             0000224801465445  
       BEGIN                                                            0000224901465451  
         CLAVTRAN := STRING(IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND),4);                   0000225001465461  
         TIPOMOVI := STRING(IDFESQOCUR(ALOG,GIND),1);                   0000225101465471  
         CLAVTIPOMOVI := CLAVTRAN !! TIPOMOVI;                          0000225201465481  
         MOVCVETIPO:= DECIMAL(CLAVTIPOMOVI);                            0000225301465491  
         CVETRA := MOVCVETIPO;                                          0000225401465501  
         ENVIOEDC:= IADFINDEDC(CVETRA);                                 0000225501465511  
       END                                                              0000225601465521  
    ELSE                                                                0000225701465531  
    ENVIOEDC:= IADFINDEDC(CVETRA);                                      0000225801465541  
    IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                             0000225901465551  
       IF INDI LEQ NMOV AND ENVIOEDC NEQ 5 THEN                         0000226001465561  
         IF FUNCION = 19 THEN                                           0000226101465562  
            BEGIN                                                       0000226201465563  
            REPLACE DCVEMDE1(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,        0000226301465566  
                                   SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000226401465568  
                                   CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000226501465570  
                                   AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000226601465572  
                                   CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000226701465574  
                                   REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,           0000226801465576  
                                  (IMPORTE DIV  100000000)              0000226901465578  
                                           MOD  100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS, 0000227001465580  
                                   IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000227101465582  
                                   HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,           0000227201465584  
                                   ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS;           0000227301465586  
            REPLACE IMONED1(INDI)      BY MONEDA    FOR 4 DIGITS;       0000227401465588  
            REPLACE IBANCOSORDE1(INDI) BY IBANCOSOI FOR 10 DIGITS;      0000227501465590  
            REPLACE DLEYEN1 (INDI) BY LEYENDA(NUM-1) FOR  80;           0000227601465592  
            REPLACE SISTEMAORI (INDI) BY SISORI FOR 4 DIGITS;           0000227701465594  
            REPLACE DAUTS151    BY AUTS151  FOR 8  DIGITS;              0000227801465596  
            INDI:= * + 1;                                               0000227901465598  
            END                                                         0000228001465600  
         ELSE                                                           0000228101465602  
         BEGIN                                                          0000228201465604  
%-MOVIL-INI                                                             0000228301465606  
           IF FUNCION = 1 THEN                                          0000228401465608  
              BEGIN                                                     0000228501465610  
              REPLACE DCVEMDE2(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,      0000228601465612  
                                        SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,      0000228701465614  
                                        CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,      0000228801465616  
                                        AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,      0000228901465618  
                                        CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,      0000229001465620  
                                        REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,      0000229101465622  
                                       (IMPORTE DIV  100000000)         0000229201465624  
                                           MOD  100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS, 0000229301465626  
                                        IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,      0000229401465628  
                                        HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,      0000229501465630  
                                        FECHAOP FOR 8 DIGITS,           0000229601465632  
                                        ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS;      0000229701465634  
              REPLACE IMONED2(INDI)      BY MONEDA    FOR 4 DIGITS;     0000229801465636  
              REPLACE IBANCOSORDE2(INDI) BY IBANCOSOI FOR 10 DIGITS;    0000229901465638  
              REPLACE DLEYEN2 (INDI) BY LEYENDA(NUM-1) FOR  80;         0000230001465640  
              REPLACE DFECHOP (INDI) BY FECHAOP FOR 8 DIGITS;           0000230101465642  
              REPLACE DAUTS151    BY AUTS151  FOR 8  DIGITS;            0000230201465644  
              INDI:= * + 1;                                             0000230301465646  
              END                                                       0000230401465648  
%-MOVIL-FIN                                                             0000230501465650  
           ELSE                                                         0000230601465652  
             BEGIN                                                      0000230701465654  
               REPLACE DCVEMDE(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,      0000230801465656  
                                        SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,      0000230901465658  
                                        CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,      0000231001465660  
                                        AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,      0000231101465662  
                                        CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,      0000231201465664  
                                        REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,      0000231301465666  
                                       (IMPORTE DIV  100000000)         0000231401465668

 PROCEDURE BUSCA_REG_AELIM;                                             0000242001470181  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000242101470191  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000242201470201

       SI_HUBO:=FALSE                                                   0000242401470221  
    ELSE                                                                0000242501470231  
      IF READ(MOVS[NUM_FILE][IDFAUTELIM(ALOG),SYNCHRONIZE],75,ALOG) THEN0000242601470241  
          SI_HUBO:=FALSE                                                0000242701470251  
       ELSE BEGIN                                                       0000242801470261  
          SUCURSAL  :=  IDFSUCOPE (ALOG);                               0000242901470271

          AUTORIZA  :=  IDFAUTAPLI(ALOG);                               0000243201470301  
          REFER     :=  IDFREFMOV (ALOG);                               0000243301470311  
          MONEDA    :=  IDFMONEDA (ALOG);                               0000243401470321  
          ESTATUS   :=  6               ;                               0000243501470331  
          HORA      :=  IDFHORAOPE(ALOG);                               0000243601470341  
          REPLACE LEYENDA(0) BY " " FOR 200;                            0000243701470351

          IMPORTE := IDFIMPOCUR(ALOG,1);                                0000243901470371  
          WHILE IDFAUTANT(ALOG) > 0 DO                                  0000244001470381  
          BEGIN                                                         0000244101470391

           THEN                                                         0000244301470411  
                SI_HUBO:=FALSE                                          0000244401470421  
             ELSE BEGIN                                                 0000244501470431  
                CVETRA  := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,1);                          0000244601470441  
                IMPORTE := IDFIMPOCUR(ALOG,1);                          0000244701470451  
             END;                                                       0000244801470461  
          END;                                                          0000244901470471  
       END;                                                             0000245001470481  
 END;                                                                   0000245101470491  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000245201470501  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_IMP_ADIC;                                       0000245301470511  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000245401470521  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000245501470531  
    INTEGER RSLT_NEXT,IND;                                              0000245601470541  
    FIND_IMP_ADIC:= 0;                                                  0000245701470551  
                                                                        0000245801470561  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000245901470571  
       RSLT_NEXT := 7                                                   0000246001470581  
    ELSE                                                                0000246101470591  
    BEGIN                                                               0000246201470601  
    CASE NUMDIA OF                                                      0000246301470611  
    BEGIN                                                               0000246401470621  
         0:BEGIN                                                        0000246501470631  
              FIND B02SXIMPADI AT B02-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151:DMERR;      0000246601470641  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000246701470651  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000246801470661  
              ELSE                                                      0000246901470671  
              BEGIN                                                     0000247001470681

                 GET S151B02IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B02-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000247301470711  
                                   IND_LEY[IND] :=B02-IND-LEY   [IND+1],0000247401470721

              END;                                                      0000247601470741  
           END;                                                         0000247701470751  
         1:BEGIN                                                        0000247801470761  
              FIND B12SXIMPADI AT B12-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000247901470771  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000248001470781  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000248101470791  
              ELSE                                                      0000248201470801  
              BEGIN                                                     0000248301470811  
                 IND := -1 ;                                            0000248401470821  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000248501470831  
                 GET S151B12IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B12-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000248601470841  
                                   IND_LEY[IND] :=B12-IND-LEY   [IND+1],0000248701470851  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B12-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000248801470861  
              END;                                                      0000248901470871  
           END;                                                         0000249001470881  
         2:BEGIN                                                        0000249101470891  
              FIND B22SXIMPADI AT B22-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000249201470901  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000249301470911  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000249401470921  
              ELSE                                                      0000249501470931  
              BEGIN                                                     0000249601470941  
                 IND := -1 ;                                            0000249701470951

              END;                                                      0000250201473001  
           END;                                                         0000250301473011

         3:BEGIN                                                        0000250501473031  
              FIND B32SXIMPADI AT B32-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000250601473041  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000250701473051  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000250801473061  
              ELSE                                                      0000250901473071  
              BEGIN                                                     0000251001473081  
                 IND := -1 ;                                            0000251101473091

                 GET S151B32IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B32-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000251301473111  
                                   IND_LEY[IND] :=B32-IND-LEY   [IND+1],0000251401473121  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B32-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000251501473131  
              END;                                                      0000251601473141

         4:BEGIN                                                        0000251801473161  
              FIND B42SXIMPADI AT B42-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000251901473171  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000252001473181  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000252101473191

              BEGIN                                                     0000252301473211

 FUNCION :=IFUNCION  ;                                                  0000254701473561  
 FECCONS :=IFECAPLI  ;                                                  0000254801473571  
 SISTEMA :=ISISTEMA  ;                                                  0000254901473581  
 PRODUCT :=IPRODUCT  ;                                                  0000255001473591  
 INSTRUM :=IDINSTR   ;                                                  0000255101473601  
 MDA     :=IDCVEMDA  ;                                                  0000255201473611

 INDMOVS :=IDINDMOVS ;                                                  0000255701473661

    AUTS151 := * -1                                                     0000255901473681  
 ELSE                                                                   0000256001473691  
    IF INDMOVS = 0 THEN                                                 0000256101473701  
    ELSE                                                                0000256201473711  
       ERROR:=15 ;                                                      0000256301473721  
                                                                        0000256401473731  
 IF ERROR = 0 THEN                                                      0000256501473741  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000256601473751  
    I := -1 ;                                                           0000256701473761  
    BOOL_I := FALSE;                                                    0000256801473771  
    WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_I DO                                0000256901473781  
    IF FECCONS = FEC_DIA[I] THEN                                        0000257001473791  
    BEGIN                                                               0000257101473801  
       NUM_FILE := I;                                                   0000257201473811  
       IF I > 4 THEN I := * - 5 ;                                       0000257301473821  
       NUMDIA := I ;                                                    0000257401473831  
       BOOL_I := TRUE;                                                  0000257501473841  
    END;                                                                0000257601473851  
    IF NOT BOOL_I THEN                                                  0000257701473861  
       ERROR := 4;                                                      0000257801473871  
    IF MDA > 0 THEN BOOL_MDA := TRUE;                                   0000257901473881  
 END;                                                                   0000258001473891  
                                                                        0000258101473901  
 IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                       0000258201473911  
    ERROR := 7;                                                         0000258301473921  
                                                                        0000258401473931  
 IF ERROR = 0 THEN                                                      0000258501473941  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000258601473951  
    RESET(CON_PLATINO);                                                 0000258701473961  
    IF BOOL_MDA THEN                                                    0000258801473971  
      ERROR:=FIND_FIRST_MOVMDA                                          0000258901473981  
    ELSE                                                                0000259001473991  
      ERROR:=FIND_FIRST_MOVCTO;                                         0000259101480011  
 END;                                                                   0000259201480021  
                                                                        0000259301480031  
 IF ERROR = 0 THEN                                                      0000259401480041  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000259501480051  
    SIGUE:=TRUE;                                                        0000259601480061  
    INDI :=0;                                                           0000259701480071  
    IF NMOV > 0 AND NMOV < 31   THEN                                    0000259801480081  
    WHILE SIGUE AND INDI LEQ NMOV DO                                    0000259901480091  
    BEGIN                                                               0000260001480101  
       IF GET_DATOS = 0 THEN                                            0000260101480111  
       BEGIN                                                            0000260201480121  
%<INICIO-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)        0000260301480122

          NUM_PROC = 41 OR NUM_PROC = 42 OR NUM_PROC = 43) THEN         0000260501480124  
         ELSE                                                           0000260601480126  
         BEGIN                                                          0000260701480127  
%<FIN-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)           0000260801480128  
          OBTIENE_LEYENDAS;                                             0000260901480131  
          NUM:=0;                                                       0000261001480141  
          IF INDMOVS = 0 THEN                                           0000261101480151  
             IF ESTATUS = 6 THEN                                        0000261201480161  
                INDIMP := 0                                             0000261301480201

             IF FIND_IMP_ADIC > 0 THEN                                  0000261701480251  
             ELSE                                                       0000261801480261  
             BEGIN                                                      0000261901480271  
                IF INDMOVS > 0 THEN                                     0000262001480281  
                   INDICE:=INDMOVS - 2                                  0000262101480291  
                ELSE INDICE := -1;                                      0000262201480301  
                INDMOVS:=0;                                             0000262301480311  
                WHILE INDICE:=*+1 LEQ 3    AND                          0000262401480321  
                      CVE_ADIC[INDICE] > 0 AND                          0000262501480331  
                      INDI LEQ NMOV DO                                  0000262601480341  
                BEGIN                                                   0000262701480351  
                   CVETRA :=  CVE_ADIC[INDICE];                         0000262801480361  
                   IMPORTE:=  IMP_ADIC[INDICE];                         0000262901480371  
                   NUM:=INDICE+2;                                       0000263001480381  
                   REPLACE_DATOS;                                       0000263101480391  
                END;                                                    0000263201480401  
             END;                                                       0000263301480411  
         END; %FIN DE IF BD_NAME                                        0000263401480421  
       END;                                                             0000263501480423  
       IF BOOL_MDA THEN                                                 0000263601480431  
         FIND_NEXT_MOVMDA                                               0000263701480441  
       ELSE                                                             0000263801480451  
         FIND_NEXT_MOVCTO;                                              0000263901480461  
       IF NOT SIGUE THEN                                                0000264001480471  
       ELSE                                                             0000264101480481  
          IF INDI+1 > NMOV THEN                                         0000264201480491  
             SIGUE:=FALSE ;                                             0000264301480501  
    END;                                                                0000264401480511  
 END;                                                                   0000264501480521  
 IF INDI = 0 AND ERROR = 0 THEN                                         0000264601480531  
    ERROR:=2;                                                           0000264701480541  
 IF INDI < NMOV THEN                                                    0000264801480551  
      REPLACE DNUMMOVS BY INDI FOR 2 DIGITS ;                           0000264901480561  
                                                                        0000265001480571
