%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000308801867011  
 PROCEDURE REPLACE_DATOS;                                               0000308901868011  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000309001869011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000309101870011  
    POINTER PTR_EBCDIC ;                                                0000309201871011  
    PICTURE PIC_IMP(" "Z(2)DZ(3)DZ(3)DZ(3)DZ(3)I99);                    0000309301871111  
% IBM <INICIO>  JGVD - NUEVO CAMPO NOMBRE ORD, BANCO EMISOR             0000309401871121  
    REPLACE DNOMORD(INDI) BY NOMORD FOR 60;                             0000309501871131  
    REPLACE DBCOEMI(INDI) BY BCOEMI FOR 40;                             0000309601871141  
% IBM <FIN>     JGVD - NUEVO CAMPO NOMBRE ORD, BANCO EMISOR             0000309701871151  
    IF ESTATUS = 6 THEN                                                 0000309801872011  
    ELSE                                                                0000309901873011  
       IMPORTE := IMPORTE*100;                                          0000310001874011  
    IF NUM LEQ 5 THEN                                                   0000310101875011  
       REPLACE DINDMOVS    BY   NUM FOR 2 DIGITS                        0000310201876011  
    ELSE                                                                0000310301877011  
       REPLACE DINDMOVS    BY   0   FOR 2 DIGITS;                       0000310401878011  
    IF BANDERA_1133  THEN                                               0000310501878021  
                                                                        0000310601878031  
    ELSE                                                                0000310701878041  
    IF SISTEMA = 500 AND                                                0000310801878111  
      (CVETRA  = 1134 OR                                                0000310901878133  
       CVETRA  = 1135 OR                                                0000311001878135  
       CVETRA  = 1136 OR                                                0000311101878137  
       CVETRA  = 1137 OR                                                0000311201878139  
       CVETRA  = 2227 OR                                                0000311301878149  
       CVETRA  = 2228) THEN                                             0000311401878151  
       REFER:= MREFCECOBAN                                              0000311501878161  
    ELSE                                                                0000311601878211  
       IF SISTEMA  = 500   AND                                          0000311701878261  
         (CVETRA   = 1133  OR                                           0000311801878271  
          CVETRA   = 2226) THEN                                         0000311901878361  
          REFER := IDFREFCTE;                                           0000312001878711  
                                                                        0000312101878811  
    IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                             0000312201879011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000312301880011  
         CLAVTRAN := STRING(IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND),4);                   0000312401881011  
         TIPOMOVI := STRING(IDFESQOCUR(ALOG,GIND),1);                   0000312501882011  
         CLAVTIPOMOVI := CLAVTRAN !! TIPOMOVI;                          0000312601883011  
         MOVCVETIPO:= DECIMAL(CLAVTIPOMOVI);                            0000312701884011  
         CVETRA := MOVCVETIPO;                                          0000312801885011  
         ENVIOEDC:= IADFINDEDC(CVETRA);                                 0000312901886011  
       END                                                              0000313001887011  
    ELSE                                                                0000313101888011  
    ENVIOEDC:= IADFINDEDC(CVETRA);                                      0000313201889011  
    IF INDI LEQ NMOV AND ENVIOEDC NEQ 5 AND                             0000313301889036  
%INICIO CJMG TESOFE 2019                                                0000313401889056  
       ((CVETRA > 730 AND CVETRA < 747) OR                              0000313501889061  
       (CVETRA > 857 AND CVETRA < 870)) AND                             0000313601889071  
%FIN CJMG TESOFE 2019                                                   0000313701889081  
       SISTEMA=500                      AND                             0000313801889086  
       FORMATO_CAT = 1                  THEN                            0000313901889111  
       BEGIN                                                            0000314001889136  
% EDITA IMPORTE Y LO MUEVE A LEYENDA, DEJANDO EL ORIGINAL EN CERO       0000314101889161  
          REPLACE AIMP BY IMPORTE FOR 16 DIGITS;                        0000314201889186  
          REPLACE AIMP_EDIT BY AIMP WITH PIC_IMP;                       0000314301889211  
          IMPORTE_ANT := IMPORTE;                                       0000314401889231  
          IMPORTE := 0;                                                 0000314501889236  
          REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY " "            FOR  40;              0000314601889246  
          REPLACE LEYENDX(INDI) BY AIMP_EDIT FOR 22;                    0000314701889256  
          REPLACE DCVEMDE(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000314801889261  
                                   SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000314901889286  
                                   CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000315001889311  
                                   AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000315101889336  
                                   CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000315201889361  
                                   REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,           0000315301889386  
                                  (IMPORTE DIV  100000000)              0000315401889411  
                                           MOD  100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS, 0000315501889436  
                                   IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000315601889461  
                                   HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,           0000315701889486  
                                   ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS;           0000315801889511  
          REPLACE DAUTS151    BY AUTS151  FOR 8  DIGITS;                0000315901889536  
          INDI:= * + 1;                                                 0000316001889911  
       END                                                              0000316101889936  
    ELSE                                                                0000316201889971  
    IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                             0000316301890011  
       IF INDI LEQ NMOV AND ENVIOEDC NEQ 5 THEN                         0000316401891011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000316501892011  
          REPLACE DCVEMDE(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000316601893011  
                                   SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000316701894011  
                                   CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,           0000316801895011  
                                   AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000316901896011  
                                   CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,           0000317001897011  
                                   REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,           0000317101898011  
                                  (IMPORTE DIV  100000000)              0000317201899011  
                                           MOD  100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS, 0000317301900011  
                                   IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,           0000317401901011  
                                   HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,           0000317501902011  
                                   ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS;           0000317601903011  
          REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY LEYENDA(NUM-1) FOR  80;              0000317701904011  
          REPLACE DAUTS151    BY AUTS151  FOR 8  DIGITS;                0000317801905011  
          INDI:= * + 1;                                                 0000317901906011  
       END                                                              0000318001907011  
       ELSE                                                             0000318101908011  
                                                                        0000318201909011  
    ELSE                                                                0000318301910011  
    IF INDI LEQ NMOV AND (ENVIOEDC NEQ 5 OR CVETRA=1009 OR              0000318401911011  
                                            CVETRA=1072) THEN           0000318501912011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000318601913011  
       REPLACE DCVEMDE(INDI) BY CVEMDE     FOR   2 DIGITS,              0000318701914011  
                                SUCURSAL   FOR   4 DIGITS,              0000318801915011  
                                CAJA       FOR   2 DIGITS,              0000318901916011  
                                AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,              0000319001917011  
                                CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,              0000319101918011  
                                REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,              0000319201919011  
                               (IMPORTE DIV  100000000)                 0000319301920011  
                                        MOD  100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS,    0000319401921011  
                                IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,              0000319501922011  
                                HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,              0000319601923011  
                                ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS;              0000319701924011  
          REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY LEYENDA(NUM-1) FOR  80;              0000319801925011  
          IF BANDERA_1133 THEN                                          0000319901925013  
             REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY LEY_1133       FOR 80             0000320001925015  
          ELSE                                                          0000320101925017  
          BEGIN                                                         0000320201925019  
%MBV INICIO CAMBIA LEYENDA                                              0000320301925021  
             REPLACE AUX_LEY      BY LEYENDA(NUM-1) FOR  80;            0000320401925026  
             IF IND_LEYTRANS = 1 AND CVETRA = 1133 THEN                 0000320501925028  
             BEGIN                                                      0000320601925041  
                REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY ARR_LEYE1      FOR 80;         0000320701925051  
                REPLACE AUX_LEY       BY DLEYEN (INDI)  FOR 80;         0000320801925061  
             END;                                                       0000320901925071  
%MBV FIN CAMBIA LEYENDA                                                 0000321001925081  
          END;                                                          0000321101925111  
          %INI - RANB                                                   0000321201925121  
      IF SIST_LIB = 500 THEN BEGIN                                      0000321301925131  
          IF NOT TAXIDCON = "          " AND CVETRATXC > 0 THEN         0000321401925141  
          BEGIN                                                         0000321501925151  
             CASE OCUIND OF                                             0000321601925161  
               BEGIN                                                    0000321701925171  
                  0:BEGIN                                               0000321801925181  
                      REPLACE LEYCOMP2(0) BY DLEYEN (INDI) FOR 80;      0000321901925191  
                      LEYTX2 := SLEYCOMP2(0);                           0000322001925201  
                      REPLACE LEYENTXC2 BY LEYTX2;                      0000322101925211  
%                 IF LEYTX2 = "                                        "0000322201925221  
%                      THEN BEGIN                                       0000322301925231  
%                         REPLACE LEYENDA2(0) BY LEYENTXC     FOR 30,   0000322401925241  
%                                         ADFTAXID(CVETRATXC) FOR 10;   0000322501925251  
%                         REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY ARR_LEYE2  FOR 80;   0000322601925261  
%                      END ELSE BEGIN                                   0000322701925271  
                         REPLACE LEYENDA3(0) BY LEYENTXC2        FOR 30,0000322801925281  
                                             ADFTAXID(CVETRATXC) FOR 10;0000322901925291  
                         REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY ARR_LEYE3 FOR  80;    0000323001925301  
%                      END;                                             0000323101925311  
                    END;                                                0000323201925321  
                  1:BEGIN                                               0000323301925331  
                      REPLACE LEYCOMP3(0) BY DLEYEN (INDI) FOR 80;      0000323401925341  
                      LEYTX3 := SLEYCOMP3(0);                           0000323501925351  
                      REPLACE LEYENTXC3 BY LEYTX3;                      0000323601925361  
%                 IF LEYTX3 = "                                        "0000323701925371  
%                      THEN BEGIN                                       0000323801925381  
%                         REPLACE LEYENDA2(0) BY LEYENTXC     FOR 30,   0000323901925391  
%                                         ADFTAXID(CVETRATXC) FOR 10;   0000324001925401  
%                         REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY ARR_LEYE2  FOR 80;   0000324101925411  
%                      END ELSE BEGIN                                   0000324201925421  
                         REPLACE LEYENDA4(0) BY LEYENTXC3        FOR 30,0000324301925431  
                                             ADFTAXID(CVETRATXC) FOR 10;0000324401925441  
                         REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY ARR_LEYE4 FOR  80;    0000324501925451  
%                      END;                                             0000324601925461  
                    END;                                                0000324701925471  
                  2:BEGIN                                               0000324801925481  
                      REPLACE LEYCOMP4(0) BY DLEYEN (INDI) FOR 80;      0000324901925491  
                      LEYTX4 := SLEYCOMP4(0);                           0000325001925501  
                      REPLACE LEYENTXC4 BY LEYTX4;                      0000325101925511  
%                 IF LEYTX4 = "                                        "0000325201925521  
%                      THEN BEGIN                                       0000325301925531  
%                         REPLACE LEYENDA2(0) BY LEYENTXC     FOR 30,   0000325401925541  
%                                         ADFTAXID(CVETRATXC) FOR 10;   0000325501925551  
%                         REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY ARR_LEYE2  FOR 80;   0000325601925561  
%                      END ELSE BEGIN                                   0000325701925571  
                         REPLACE LEYENDA5(0) BY LEYENTXC4        FOR 30,0000325801925581  
                                             ADFTAXID(CVETRATXC) FOR 10;0000325901925591  
                         REPLACE DLEYEN (INDI) BY ARR_LEYE5 FOR  80;    0000326001925601

                    END;                                                0000326201925621  
                  3:BEGIN                                               0000326301925631

                      REPLACE LEYENTXC5 BY LEYTX5;                      0000326601925661

                    END;                                                0000327701925771

%---------------------------------------------------------------------  0000329101931011  
 PROCEDURE OBTIENE_LEYENDAS;                                            0000329201932011  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------  0000329301933011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000329401934011  
   INTEGER M;                                                           0000329501935011  
%MBV INICIO VAR AUX PARA CONCATENAR REF NUM                             0000329601935021  
   EBCDIC ARRAY EBC_ARR_REFNUM[0:12];                                   0000329701935031  
          ARRAY ARR_REFNUM     [0:2];                                   0000329801935041  
                                                                        0000329901935051  
%MBV FIN VAR AUX PARA CONCATENAR REF NUM                                0000330001935061  
                                                                        0000330101935062  
   PROCEDURE ANALIZA(TAMANO,CAMPO_ALFA,CAMPO_NUM,CAMPO_STR,TIPO);       0000330201935111  
   REFERENCE TAMANO, CAMPO_ALFA, CAMPO_NUM, CAMPO_STR, TIPO;            0000330301935121  
   DOUBLE       CAMPO_NUM     ;                                         0000330401935131  
   ARRAY        CAMPO_ALFA[*] ;                                         0000330501935141  
   STRING       CAMPO_STR     ;                                         0000330601935151  
   BOOLEAN      TIPO          ;                                         0000330701935161  
   INTEGER      TAMANO        ;                                         0000330801935171  
   BEGIN                                                                0000330901935181  
      INTEGER   LONG_DATO, CONT_NUMEROS;                                0000331001935191  
      STRING    STR_CEROS;                                              0000331101935201  
      TIPO      := FALSE;                                               0000331201935211  
      CAMPO_STR := STRING(CAMPO_ALFA,TAMANO);                           0000331301935221  
      CAMPO_STR := HEAD(TAIL(CAMPO_STR," "),NOT " ");                   0000331401935231  
      TAMANO    := LENGTH(CAMPO_STR);                                   0000331501935241  
      REPLACE POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) BY " " FOR SIZE(CAMPO_ALFA);          0000331601935251  
      REPLACE POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) BY CAMPO_STR;                         0000331701935261  
                                                                        0000331801935271  
      SCAN POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) FOR CONT_NUMEROS:TAMANO                  0000331901935281  
                               WHILE IN NUMEROS;                        0000332001935291  
      CONT_NUMEROS := TAMANO - CONT_NUMEROS;                            0000332101935301  
      IF LENGTH(CAMPO_STR) > 0 THEN                                     0000332201935303  
      BEGIN                                                             0000332301935305  
         TAMANO    := LENGTH(CAMPO_STR);                                0000332401935311  
         IF CONT_NUMEROS = TAMANO THEN                                  0000332501935321  
         BEGIN                                                          0000332601935331  
            TIPO      := TRUE ;                                         0000332701935341  
            STR_CEROS := HEAD(CAMPO_STR,"0");                           0000332801935351  
            IF LENGTH(STR_CEROS) < TAMANO THEN                          0000332901935361  
            BEGIN                                                       0000333001935371  
               CAMPO_STR := DROP(CAMPO_STR, LENGTH(STR_CEROS));         0000333101935381  
               CAMPO_NUM := DECIMAL(CAMPO_STR);                         0000333201935391  
               TAMANO    := LENGTH(CAMPO_STR);                          0000333301935401  
            END                                                         0000333401935411  
            ELSE                                                        0000333501935421  
            BEGIN                                                       0000333601935431  
               TAMANO    := 1;                                          0000333701935441  
               CAMPO_STR := "0";                                        0000333801935451  
               CAMPO_NUM := 0                                           0000333901935461  
            END;                                                        0000334001935471  
         END                                                            0000334101935481  
         ELSE                                                           0000334201935491  
         BEGIN                                                          0000334301935501  
            CAMPO_NUM := 0;                                             0000334401935511  
            SCAN POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) FOR CONT_NUMEROS:TAMANO            0000334501935521  
                                     WHILE > 48"00";                    0000334601935531  
            TAMANO := TAMANO - CONT_NUMEROS;                            0000334701935541  
            IF TAMANO = 0 THEN CAMPO_STR := 48"00";                     0000334801935551  
         END;                                                           0000334901935561  
      END                                                               0000335001935563  
      ELSE                                                              0000335101935565  
      BEGIN                                                             0000335201935566

  PROCEDURE REF_1133;                                                   0000335801935582  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000335901935584

                STR_REF,TIPO_DATO1)                ;                    0000338101936098

                                                                        0000338301936101  
        ELSE                                                            0000338401936102

                                  "     "                               0000340401936371

 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_IMP_ADIC;                                       0000353701998011

    ELSE                                                                0000354502006011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000354602007011  
    CASE NUMDIA OF                                                      0000354702008011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000354802009011  
         0:BEGIN                                                        0000354902010011

                                   IND_LEY[IND] :=B02-IND-LEY   [IND+1],0000355802019011

              END;                                                      0000356002021011  
           END;                                                         0000356102022011  
         1:BEGIN                                                        0000356202023011

              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000356402025011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000356502026011  
              ELSE                                                      0000356602027011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000356702028011

              END;                                                      0000357302034011  
           END;                                                         0000357402035011  
         2:BEGIN                                                        0000357502036011

              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000357702038011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000357802039011

    BEGIN                                                               0000372002159011

       BEGIN                                                            0000372202161011

         NUM_PROC = 41 OR NUM_PROC = 42 OR NUM_PROC = 43) THEN          0000372502161014  
         ELSE                                                           0000372602161016  
         BEGIN                                                          0000372702161017  
%<FIN-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)           0000372802161018  
          OBTIENE_LEYENDAS;                                             0000372902162011  
          NUM:=0;                                                       0000373002163011  
          IF INDMOVS = 0 THEN                                           0000373102164011  
             IF ESTATUS = 6 THEN                                        0000373202165011  
             BEGIN                                                      0000373302166011  
                BUSCA_REG_AELIM ;                                       0000373402167011  
                REPLACE_DATOS;                                          0000373502168011  
                INDIMP := 0;                                            0000373602169011  
             END                                                        0000373702170011  
             ELSE                                                       0000373802171011  
                REPLACE_DATOS;                                          0000373902172011  
          IF INDIMP > 0 THEN                                            0000374002173011  
             IF FIND_IMP_ADIC > 0 THEN                                  0000374102174011  
             ELSE                                                       0000374202175011  
             BEGIN                                                      0000374302176011  
                IF INDMOVS > 0 THEN                                     0000374402177011  
                   INDICE:=INDMOVS                                      0000374502178011  
                ELSE INDICE := 0;                                       0000374602179011  
                INDMOVS:=0;                                             0000374702180011  
                WHILE INDICE LEQ 3         AND                          0000374802181011  
                      CVE_ADIC[INDICE] > 0 AND                          0000374902182011  
                      INDI <   NMOV DO                                  0000375002183011  
                BEGIN                                                   0000375102184011  
                   CVETRA :=  CVE_ADIC[INDICE];                         0000375202185011  
                   IMPORTE:=  IMP_ADIC[INDICE];                         0000375302186011  
                   NUM:=INDICE  ;                                       0000375402187011  
                   %INI - RANB                                          0000375502187111  
                   CVETRATXC  := CVETRA;                                0000375602187211  
                   TAXIDCON := SADFTAXID(CVETRATXC);                    0000375702187311  
                   OCUIND := INDICE;                                    0000375802187411  
                   %FIN - RANB                                          0000375902187511  
                   REPLACE_DATOS;                                       0000376002188011  
                   INDICE := * + 1;                                     0000376102188111  
                END;                                                    0000376202189011  
             END;                                                       0000376302190011  
         END; % FIN DE IF BD_NAME                                       0000376402191011  
       END;                                                             0000376502191021  
       INDMOVS:=0;                                                      0000376602191111  
       IF BOOL_MDA THEN                                                 0000376702192011  
         ERROR := FIND_NEXT_MOVMDA                                      0000376802193011  
       ELSE                                                             0000376902194011  
         ERROR := FIND_NEXT_MOVCTO;                                     0000377002195011  
       IF INDI+1 > NMOV OR ERROR = 5         THEN SIGUE:=FALSE ;        0000377102195061  
       IF ERROR = 5 AND CVE_ADIC[INDICE] > 0 THEN ERROR := 3;           0000377202195111  
    END;                                                                0000377302212011  
 END;                                                                   0000377402213011  
 REPLACE DNUMMOVS BY INDI FOR 2 DIGITS;                                 0000377502213111  
 IF ERROR = 20 OR ERROR = 5 THEN                                        0000377602223011  
    ERROR := 0;                                                         0000377702224011  
                                                                        0000377802225011  
%REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(ADATOS1) FOR SIZEDATOS;                       0000377902226011  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000378002227011  
$SET OMIT = OLDCODE                                                     0000378102228011  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(ADATOS1) FOR SIZEDATOS;                       0000378202229011  
$POP OMIT                                                               0000378302230011  
$SET OMIT = NOT OLDCODE                                                 0000378402231011  
 REPLACE POINTER(A2K_DATOS,4) BY POINTER(ADATOS1,4) FOR 2,              0000378502232011  
                       POINTER(ADATOS1,4)+4 FOR 6,                      0000378602233011  
                       POINTER(ADATOS1,4)+10 FOR (SIZEDATOS*2 - 8);     0000378702234011  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(A2K_DATOS) FOR SIZEDATOS;                     0000378802235011  
$POP OMIT                                                               0000378902236011  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000379002237011  
 MOVXCNT := ERROR;                                                      0000379102238011  
 CAUSE(CONXCNT);                                                        0000379202239011  
 END OF MOVXCNT;                                                        0000379302240011  
$SET PAGE                                                               0000379402241011

%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000391902366011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE VALIDACVE ;                                          0000392002367011  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000392102368011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000392202369011  
 VALIDACVE : = 0 ;                                                      0000392302370011  
 IF MONEDA = MONANT THEN                                                0000392402371011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000392502372011  
    IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                             0000392602373011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000392702374011  
         CLAVTRAN := STRING(IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND),4);                   0000392802375011  
         TIPOMOVI := STRING(IDFESQOCUR(ALOG,GIND),1);                   0000392902376011  
         CLAVTIPOMOVI := CLAVTRAN !! TIPOMOVI;                          0000393002377011  
         MOVCVETIPO:= DECIMAL(CLAVTIPOMOVI);                            0000393102378011  
         CVETRA := MOVCVETIPO;                                          0000393202379011  
         NATURALEZA:= IADFCVECOL(CVETRA) ;                              0000393302380011  
       END                                                              0000393402381011  
    ELSE                                                                0000393502382011  
    NATURALEZA:= IADFCVECOL(CVETRA) ;                                   0000393602383011  
                                                                        0000393702384011  
    IF ((NATCGO = 1 AND  NATURALEZA = 1 )  OR                           0000393802385011  
        (NATABO = 1 AND  NATURALEZA = 2 )) AND                          0000393902386011  
       INT_BCOS = BANCOS THEN                                           0000394002387011  
        VALIDACVE := 1 ;                                                0000394102388011  
 END                                                                    0000394202389011  
 ELSE                                                                   0000394302390011  
    IF MONANT   = 0 AND                                                 0000394402391011  
       INT_BCOS = BANCOS THEN                                           0000394502392011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000394602393011  
       IF FORMATO_CAT = 3 THEN                                          0000394702394011  
          BEGIN                                                         0000394802395011  
            CLAVTRAN := STRING(IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND),4);                0000394902396011  
            TIPOMOVI := STRING(IDFESQOCUR(ALOG,GIND),1);                0000395002397011  
            CLAVTIPOMOVI := CLAVTRAN !! TIPOMOVI;                       0000395102398011  
            MOVCVETIPO:= DECIMAL(CLAVTIPOMOVI);                         0000395202399011  
            CVETRA := MOVCVETIPO;                                       0000395302400011  
            NATURALEZA:= IADFCVECOL(CVETRA) ;                           0000395402401011  
            VALIDACVE := 1;                                             0000395502402011  
          END                                                           0000395602403011  
       ELSE                                                             0000395702404011  
       NATURALEZA:=IADFCVECOL(CVETRA) ;                                 0000395802405011  
       VALIDACVE := 1;                                                  0000395902406011  
    END;                                                                0000396002407011  
%                                                                       0000396102408011  
 END VALIDACVE ;                                                        0000396202409011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000396302410011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_FIRST_MOVTO;                                    0000396402411011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000396502412011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000396602413011  
                                                                        0000396702414011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000396802415011  
       ERROR := 7                                                       0000396902416011  
    ELSE                                                                0000397002417011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000397102418011  
 CASE NUMDIA OF                                                         0000397202419011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000397302420011  
     0:FIND FIRST B01BXMOVCAJ AT B01-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000397402421011  
                                 B01-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000397502422011  
                                 B01-MOV-AUTS151  > CONTROL :DMERR;     0000397602423011  
                                                                        0000397702424011  
     1:FIND FIRST B11BXMOVCAJ AT B11-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000397802425011  
                                 B11-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000397902426011  
                                 B11-MOV-AUTS151  > CONTROL :DMERR;     0000398002427011  
                                                                        0000398102428011  
     2:FIND FIRST B21BXMOVCAJ AT B21-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000398202429011  
                                 B21-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000398302430011  
                                 B21-MOV-AUTS151  > CONTROL :DMERR;     0000398402431011  
                                                                        0000398502432011  
     3:FIND FIRST B31BXMOVCAJ AT B31-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000398602433011  
                                 B31-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000398702434011  
                                 B31-MOV-AUTS151  > CONTROL :DMERR;     0000398802435011  
                                                                        0000398902436011  
     4:FIND FIRST B41BXMOVCAJ AT B41-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000399002437011  
                                 B41-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000399102438011  
                                 B41-MOV-AUTS151  > CONTROL :DMERR;     0000399202439011  
     ELSE:                                                              0000399302440011  
         BEGIN                                                          0000399402441011  
         ERROR := 4 ;                                                   0000399502442011  
         END;                                                           0000399602443011  
 END;                                                                   0000399702444011  
 IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                                 0000399802445011  
    IF DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                                    0000399902446011  
       ERROR := 1                                                       0000400002447011  
    ELSE                                                                0000400102448011  
       ERRORDB  ;                                                       0000400202449011  
 END;                                                                   0000400302450011  
 FIND_FIRST_MOVTO := ERROR;                                             0000400402451011  
                                                                        0000400502452011  
 END OF FIND_FIRST_MOVTO;                                               0000400602453011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000400702454011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_NEXT_MOVTO;                                     0000400802455011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000400902456011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000401002457011  
                                                                        0000401102458011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000401202459011  
       FIND_NEXT_MOVTO := 7                                             0000401302460011  
    ELSE                                                                0000401402461011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000401502462011  
    CASE NUMDIA OF                                                      0000401602463011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000401702464011  
       0:FIND NEXT B01BXMOVCAJ AT B01-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000401802465011  
                                  B01-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000401902466011  
                                                                        0000402002467011  
       1:FIND NEXT B11BXMOVCAJ AT B11-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000402102468011  
                                  B11-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000402202469011  
                                                                        0000402302470011  
       2:FIND NEXT B21BXMOVCAJ AT B21-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000402402471011  
                                  B21-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000402502472011  
                                                                        0000402602473011  
       3:FIND NEXT B31BXMOVCAJ AT B31-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000402702474011  
                                  B31-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000402802475011  
                                                                        0000402902476011  
       4:FIND NEXT B41BXMOVCAJ AT B41-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000403002477011  
                                  B41-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000403102478011  
    ELSE:ERROR := 4                                                     0000403202479011  
    END;                                                                0000403302480011  
    IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                              0000403402481011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000403502482011  
       SIGUE:=FALSE;                                                    0000403602483011  
       FIND_NEXT_MOVTO:= 5;                                             0000403702484011  
    END;                                                                0000403802485011  
    END;                                                                0000403902486011  
%   FIND_NEXT_MOVTO:=ERROR;                                             0000404002487011  
                                                                        0000404102488011  
 END OF FIND_NEXT_MOVTO;                                                0000404202489011  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000404302490011  
 PROCEDURE REPLACE_DATOS;                                               0000404402491011  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000404502492011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000404602493011  
    IMPORTE := IMPORTE*100;                                             0000404702494011  
    REPLACE DCVEMDE(INDI) BY 0          FOR   2 DIGITS,                 0000404802495011  
                             AUTORIZA   FOR   8 DIGITS,                 0000404902496011  
                             SUCPROM    FOR   4 DIGITS,                 0000405002497011  
                            (CUENTA DIV  1000000)                       0000405102498011  
                                    MOD  1000000 FOR 6 DIGITS,          0000405202499011  
                             CUENTA     FOR   6 DIGITS,                 0000405302500011  
                             CVETRA     FOR   4 DIGITS,                 0000405402501011  
                             0          FOR   2 DIGITS,                 0000405502502011  
                             MONEDA     FOR   4 DIGITS,                 0000405602503011  
                             REFER      FOR  16 DIGITS,                 0000405702504011  
                            (IMPORTE DIV  100000000)                    0000405802505011  
                                     MOD  100000000 FOR 8 DIGITS,       0000405902506011  
                             IMPORTE    FOR   8 DIGITS,                 0000406002507011  
                             HORA       FOR   6 DIGITS,                 0000406102508011  
                             ESTATUS    FOR   2 DIGITS,                 0000406202509011  
                             NATURALEZA FOR   2 DIGITS,                 0000406302510011  
                             SUCTR      FOR   4 DIGITS,                 0000406402511011  
                             INST       FOR   2 DIGITS,                 0000406502512011  
%                            FECVAL     FOR   6 DIGITS;                 0000406602513011  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000406702514011  
                             FECVAL     FOR   8 DIGITS;                 0000406802515011  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000406902516011  
    REPLACE DAUTAPL     BY AUTORIZA FOR 8  DIGITS;                      0000407002517011  
    REPLACE DAUTADM     BY CONTROL  FOR 8  DIGITS;                      0000407102518011  
    INDI:= * + 1;                                                       0000407202519011  
    REPLACE DNMOV       BY INDI     FOR 2  DIGITS;                      0000407302520011  
 END OF REPLACE_DATOS;                                                  0000407402521011

 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_IMP_ADIC;                                       0000421002652011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000421102653011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000421202654011  
    INTEGER RSLT_NEXT,IND;                                              0000421302655011  
    FIND_IMP_ADIC:= 0;                                                  0000421402656011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000421502657011  
       RSLT_NEXT := 7                                                   0000421602658011  
    ELSE                                                                0000421702659011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000421802660011  
    CASE NUMDIA OF                                                      0000421902661011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000422002662011  
         0:BEGIN                                                        0000422102663011  
              FIND B02SXIMPADI AT B02-MOV-AUTS151 = CONTROL:DMERR;      0000422202664011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000422302665011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000422402666011  
              ELSE                                                      0000422502667011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000422602668011  
                 IND := -1 ;                                            0000422702669011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000422802670011  
                 GET S151B02IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B02-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000422902671011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B02-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000423002672011  
              END;                                                      0000423102673011  
           END;                                                         0000423202674011  
         1:BEGIN                                                        0000423302675011  
              FIND B12SXIMPADI AT B12-MOV-AUTS151 = CONTROL:DMERR;      0000423402676011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000423502677011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000423602678011  
              ELSE                                                      0000423702679011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000423802680011  
                 IND := -1 ;                                            0000423902681011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000424002682011  
                 GET S151B12IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B12-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000424102683011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B12-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000424202684011  
              END;                                                      0000424302685011  
           END;                                                         0000424402686011  
         2:BEGIN                                                        0000424502687011  
              FIND B22SXIMPADI AT B22-MOV-AUTS151 = CONTROL:DMERR;      0000424602688011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000424702689011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000424802690011  
              ELSE                                                      0000424902691011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000425002692011  
                 IND := -1;                                             0000425102693011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000425202694011  
                 GET S151B22IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B22-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000425302695011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B22-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000425402696011  
              END;                                                      0000425502697011  
           END;                                                         0000425602698011  
         3:BEGIN                                                        0000425702699011  
              FIND B32SXIMPADI AT B32-MOV-AUTS151 = CONTROL:DMERR;      0000425802700011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000425902701011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000426002702011  
              ELSE                                                      0000426102703011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000426202704011  
                 IND := -1;                                             0000426302705011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000426402706011  
                 GET S151B32IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B32-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000426502707011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B32-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000426602708011  
              END;                                                      0000426702709011  
           END;                                                         0000426802710011  
         4:BEGIN                                                        0000426902711011  
              FIND B42SXIMPADI AT B42-MOV-AUTS151 = CONTROL:DMERR;      0000427002712011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000427102713011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000427202714011  
              ELSE                                                      0000427302715011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000427402716011  
                 IND := -1;                                             0000427502717011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000427602718011  
                 GET S151B42IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B42-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000427702719011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B42-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000427802720011  
              END;                                                      0000427902721011  
           END;                                                         0000428002722011  
      ELSE:RSLT_NEXT:=2 ;                                               0000428102723011  
    END;                                                                0000428202724011  
    FIND_IMP_ADIC := RSLT_NEXT;                                         0000428302725011  
    END;                                                                0000428402726011  
 END OF FIND_IMP_ADIC ;                                                 0000428502727011

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%  0000428702729011  
%                   OUTER BLOCK DE MOVS3NIVEL                        %  0000428802730011  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%  0000428902731011  
 ACQUIREL(E4,A4);                                                       0000429002732011  
 SIZEDATOS:= SIZE(DATOS);                                               0000429102733011  
 REPLACE POINTER(ADATOS1) BY DATOS FOR SIZEDATOS;                       0000429202734011  
                                                                        0000429302735011  
 FECCONS :=IFECAPLI ;                                                   0000429402736011  
 SUCANT  :=IDSUC     ;                                                  0000429502737011  
 CAJANT  :=IDCAJA    ;                                                  0000429602738011  
 AUTORIZA:=IDAUTAPL  ;                                                  0000429702739011  
 CONTROL :=IDAUTADM  ;                                                  0000429802740011  
%SISANT  :=IDSIST    ;                                                  0000429902741011  
 NMOV    :=IDNMOV    ;                                                  0000430002742011  
 NATCGO  :=IDNATCGO  ;                                                  0000430102743011  
 NATABO  :=IDNATABO  ;                                                  0000430202744011  
 NATICGO :=IDNATICGO ;                                                  0000430302745011  
 NATIABO :=IDNATIABO ;                                                  0000430402746011  
 MONANT  :=IDMON     ;                                                  0000430502747011  
 BANCOS  :=IDBANCOS  ;                                                  0000430602748011  
  BORIANT :=IDBCOORI  ;                                                 0000430702749011  
 BDESANT :=IDBCODES  ;                                                  0000430802750011  
 REPLACE STR_BANCOS BY 0       FOR 2 DIGITS,                            0000430902750111  
                       BORIANT FOR 3 DIGITS,                            0000431002750211  
                       0       FOR 2 DIGITS,                            0000431102750311  
                       BDESANT FOR 3 DIGITS;                            0000431202750411  
 INT_BCOS := INTEGER(STR_BANCOS,10);                                    0000431302750511  
                                                                        0000431402751011  
                                                                        0000431502752011  
 I := -1 ;                                                              0000431602753011  
 BOOL_I := FALSE;                                                       0000431702754011  
 WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_I DO                                   0000431802755011  
    IF FECCONS = FEC_DIA[I] THEN                                        0000431902756011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000432002757011  
       IF I > 4 THEN I := * - 5 ;                                       0000432102758011  
       NUMDIA := I ;                                                    0000432202759011  
       BOOL_I := TRUE;                                                  0000432302760011  
    END;                                                                0000432402761011  
                                                                        0000432502762011  
 IF NOT BOOL_I THEN                                                     0000432602763011  
    ERROR := 4;                                                         0000432702764011  
                                                                        0000432802765011  
  IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                      0000432902766011  
     ERROR := 7;                                                        0000433002767011  
 IF ERROR = 0 THEN                                                      0000433102768011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000433202769011  
 RESET(CON3NIVEL);                                                      0000433302770011  
 IF FIND_FIRST_MOVTO > 0 THEN                                           0000433402771011  
    MOVS3NIVEL:=ERROR                                                   0000433502772011  
 ELSE                                                                   0000433602773011  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000433702774011  
     SIGUE:=TRUE;                                                       0000433802775011  
     INDI :=0;                                                          0000433902776011  
     IF NMOV > 0 AND NMOV < 51   THEN                                   0000434002777011  
     WHILE SIGUE AND INDI LEQ NMOV DO                                   0000434102778011  
     BEGIN                                                              0000434202779011  
        IF GET_DATOS = 0 THEN                                           0000434302780011  
        BEGIN                                                           0000434402781011  
%<INICIO-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)        0000434502781012  
          IF SIST_LIB = 500  AND (NUM_PROC = 40 OR                      0000434602781013  
           NUM_PROC = 41 OR NUM_PROC = 42 OR NUM_PROC = 43) THEN        0000434702781014  
          ELSE                                                          0000434802781016  
          BEGIN                                                         0000434902781017  
%<FIN-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)           0000435002781018  
           REPLACE_DATOS;                                               0000435102782011  
           IF INDIMP > 0 THEN                                           0000435202783011  
              IF FIND_IMP_ADIC = 0 THEN                                 0000435302784011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000435402785011  
                 INDICE:=-1 ;                                           0000435502786011  
                 WHILE INDICE:=*+1 LEQ 3    AND                         0000435602787011  
                       INDI < NMOV          AND                         0000435702788011  
                       CVE_ADIC[INDICE] > 0 DO                          0000435802789011  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000435902790011  
                    CVETRA :=  CVE_ADIC[INDICE];                        0000436002791011  
                    IMPORTE:=  IMP_ADIC[INDICE];                        0000436102792011  
                    IF VALIDACVE = 1 THEN                               0000436202793011  
                       REPLACE_DATOS;                                   0000436302794011  
                 END;                                                   0000436402795011  
              END;                                                      0000436502796011  
          END; % FIN DEL  IF BD_NAME                                    0000436602797011  
        END;                                                            0000436702797021  
        FIND_NEXT_MOVTO;                                                0000436802798011  
        IF NOT SIGUE THEN                                               0000436902799011  
        ELSE                                                            0000437002800011  
           IF INDI+1 > NMOV THEN                                        0000437102801011  
           BEGIN                                                        0000437202802011  
              SIGUE:=FALSE ;                                            0000437302803011  
              FIND_NEXT_MOVTO;                                          0000437402804011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000437502805011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000437602806011  
                 REPLACE DAUTAPL BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS ;                    0000437702807011  
                 REPLACE DAUTADM BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS ;                    0000437802808011  
                 REPLACE DNMOV   BY INDI FOR 2 DIGITS;                  0000437902809011  
              END                                                       0000438002810011  
           END;                                                         0000438102811011  
     END;                                                               0000438202812011  
  END;                                                                  0000438302813011  
 END;                                                                   0000438402814011  
  IF INDI = 0 AND ERROR = 0 THEN                                        0000438502815011  
     ERROR:=2;                                                          0000438602816011  
  IF INDI < NMOV THEN                                                   0000438702817011  
     BEGIN                                                              0000438802818011  
       REPLACE DAUTAPL BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS ;                              0000438902819011  
       REPLACE DAUTADM BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS ;                              0000439002820011  
       REPLACE DNMOV   BY INDI FOR 2 DIGITS;                            0000439102821011  
     END ;                                                              0000439202822011  
                                                                        0000439302823011  
  IF ERROR = 20 THEN                                                    0000439402824011  
     ERROR := 0;                                                        0000439502825011  
                                                                        0000439602826011  
  REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(ADATOS1) FOR SIZEDATOS;                      0000439702827011  
 MOVS3NIVEL := ERROR;                                                   0000439802828011  
 CAUSE(CON3NIVEL);                                                      0000439902829011  
 END OF MOVS3NIVEL;                                                     0000440002830011

 INTEGER PROCEDURE TOTXCAJA(DATOS);                                     0000440302833011  
 EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                 0000440402834011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000440502835011  
%******************************************************************     0000440602836011  
 INTEGER SUCTOT,CAJATOT,MDATOT,J,FECTOT,NUMTOT,SIZETOT, MDA, ERROR;     0000440702837011  
 BOOLEAN BOOL_J;                                                        0000440802838011  
%ARRAY TOTDATOS[0:289];                                                 0000440902839011  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000441002840011  
 ARRAY TOTDATOS[0:290], STR_BANCOS[0:9];                                0000441102841011  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000441202842011  
 INTEGER SEC, IND, NCAR, ICAR, NABO,IABO;                               0000441302843011  
 INTEGER ARRAY NUMCAR,NUMABO,TNUMCAR,TNUMABO[0:29];                     0000441402844011  
 DOUBLE  ARRAY IMPCAR,IMPABO,TIMPCAR,TIMPABO[0:29];                     0000441502845011  
 DOUBLE  INT_BCOS;                                                      0000441602846011  
 DEFINE                                                                 0000441702847011  
     IFUNTOT     = INTEGER (POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)                #,       0000441802848011  
%    IFECTOT     = INTEGER((POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+  2)      , 06)#,       0000441902849011  
%    DFECTOT     =          POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+  2            #,       0000442002850011  
%    ISISTOT     = INTEGER((POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+  8)      , 04)#,       0000442102851011  
%    DSISTOT     =          POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+  8            #,       0000442202852011  
%    IPRDTOT     = INTEGER((POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+ 12)      , 04)#,       0000442302853011

%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000447502905011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_NEXT_TOTAL;                                     0000447602906011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000447702907011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000447802908011

    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000448002910011  
       RSLT_NEXT := 7                                                   0000448102911011  
    ELSE                                                                0000448202912011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000448302913011  
    CASE NUMTOT OF                                                      0000448402914011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000448502915011  
       0:                                                               0000448602916011  
           FIND NEXT B04SXCSISUCCAJ AT B04-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND    0000448702917011  
                                       B04-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND    0000448802918011  
                                       B04-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND    0000448902919011

                                       B04-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;   0000449102921011  
       1:                                                               0000449202922011  
           FIND NEXT B14SXCSISUCCAJ AT B14-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND    0000449302923011  
                                       B14-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND    0000449402924011  
                                       B14-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND    0000449502925011  
                                       B14-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND    0000449602926011  
                                       B14-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;   0000449702927011  
       2:                                                               0000449802928011  
           FIND NEXT B24SXCSISUCCAJ AT B24-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND    0000449902929011  
                                       B24-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND    0000450002930011  
                                       B24-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND    0000450102931011  
                                       B24-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND    0000450202932011  
                                       B24-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;   0000450302933011  
       3:                                                               0000450402934011  
           FIND NEXT B34SXCSISUCCAJ AT B34-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND    0000450502935011  
                                       B34-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND    0000450602936011  
                                       B34-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND    0000450702937011  
                                       B34-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND    0000450802938011  
                                       B34-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;   0000450902939011  
       4:                                                               0000451002940011  
           FIND NEXT B44SXCSISUCCAJ AT B44-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND    0000451102941011  
                                       B44-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND    0000451202942011

                                       B44-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND    0000451402944011  
                                       B44-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;   0000451502945011  
    ELSE: RSLT_NEXT:= 5;                                                0000451602946011  
    END;                                                                0000451702947011  
                                                                        0000451802948011  
    IF RSLT_NEXT = 0 THEN                                               0000451902949011  
       IF BOOLEAN(DMERR)THEN                                            0000452002950011  
          IF DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                              0000452102951011  
             RSLT_NEXT := 1                                             0000452202952011  
          ELSE                                                          0000452302953011  
          BEGIN                                                         0000452402954011  
             RSLT_NEXT:= 2;                                             0000452502955011  
             ERRORDB;                                                   0000452602956011  
          END;                                                          0000452702957011  
    END;                                                                0000452802958011  
    FIND_NEXT_TOTAL := RSLT_NEXT;                                       0000452902959011  
                                                                        0000453002960011  
 END OF FIND_NEXT_TOTAL;                                                0000453102961011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000453202962011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE GET_TOTAL;                                           0000453302963011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000453402964011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000453502965011  
 INTEGER RSLT_GETT,I_I;                                                 0000453602966011  
    CASE NUMTOT OF                                                      0000453702967011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000453802968011  
       0:BEGIN                                                          0000453902969011  
         I_I := -1 ;                                                    0000454002970011  
         WHILE I_I := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                    0000454102971011  
            GET S151B04CSISUCCAJ(NUMCAR[I_I]:=B04-NUM-CR[I_I+1],        0000454202972011  
                                 IMPCAR[I_I]:=B04-IMP-CR[I_I+1],        0000454302973011  
                                 NUMABO[I_I]:=B04-NUM-AB[I_I+1],        0000454402974011  
                                 IMPABO[I_I]:=B04-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);       0000454502975011  
         GET S151B04CSISUCCAJ(SEC := B04-MOV-SECREN);                   0000454602976011  
         SEC := SEC * 10;                                               0000454702977011  
         END;                                                           0000454802978011  
       1:BEGIN                                                          0000454902979011  
         I_I := -1 ;                                                    0000455002980011  
         WHILE I_I := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                    0000455102981011  
            GET S151B14CSISUCCAJ(NUMCAR[I_I]:=B14-NUM-CR[I_I+1],        0000455202982011  
                                 IMPCAR[I_I]:=B14-IMP-CR[I_I+1],        0000455302983011  
                                 NUMABO[I_I]:=B14-NUM-AB[I_I+1],        0000455402984011  
                                 IMPABO[I_I]:=B14-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);       0000455502985011  
         GET S151B14CSISUCCAJ(SEC := B14-MOV-SECREN);                   0000455602986011  
         SEC := SEC * 10;                                               0000455702987011  
         END;                                                           0000455802988011  
       2:BEGIN                                                          0000455902989011  
         I_I := -1 ;                                                    0000456002990011  
         WHILE I_I := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                    0000456102991011  
            GET S151B24CSISUCCAJ(NUMCAR[I_I]:=B24-NUM-CR[I_I+1],        0000456202992011  
                                 IMPCAR[I_I]:=B24-IMP-CR[I_I+1],        0000456302993011  
                                 NUMABO[I_I]:=B24-NUM-AB[I_I+1],        0000456402994011  
                                 IMPABO[I_I]:=B24-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);       0000456502995011  
         GET S151B24CSISUCCAJ(SEC := B24-MOV-SECREN);                   0000456602996011  
         SEC := SEC * 10;                                               0000456702997011  
         END;                                                           0000456802998011  
       3:BEGIN                                                          0000456902999011  
         I_I := -1 ;                                                    0000457003000011  
         WHILE I_I := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                    0000457103001011  
            GET S151B34CSISUCCAJ(NUMCAR[I_I]:=B34-NUM-CR[I_I+1],        0000457203002011  
                                 IMPCAR[I_I]:=B34-IMP-CR[I_I+1],        0000457303003011  
                                 NUMABO[I_I]:=B34-NUM-AB[I_I+1],        0000457403004011  
                                 IMPABO[I_I]:=B34-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);       0000457503005011  
         GET S151B34CSISUCCAJ(SEC := B34-MOV-SECREN);                   0000457603006011  
         SEC := SEC * 10;                                               0000457703007011  
         END;                                                           0000457803008011  
       4:BEGIN                                                          0000457903009011  
         I_I := -1 ;                                                    0000458003010011  
         WHILE I_I := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                    0000458103011011  
            GET S151B44CSISUCCAJ(NUMCAR[I_I]:=B44-NUM-CR[I_I+1],        0000458203012011  
                                 IMPCAR[I_I]:=B44-IMP-CR[I_I+1],        0000458303013011  
                                 NUMABO[I_I]:=B44-NUM-AB[I_I+1],        0000458403014011  
                                 IMPABO[I_I]:=B44-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);       0000458503015011  
         GET S151B44CSISUCCAJ(SEC := B44-MOV-SECREN);                   0000458603016011  
         SEC := SEC * 10;                                               0000458703017011  
         END;                                                           0000458803018011  
    ELSE:RSLT_GETT:=5;                                                  0000458903019011  
    END;                                                                0000459003020011  
    I_I:=-1;                                                            0000459103021011  
    IF SIST_LIB = 264 AND SEC > 10 THEN                                 0000459203022011  
                                                                        0000459303023011  
    ELSE                                                                0000459403024011  
    WHILE I_I:= * + 1 LEQ 10 DO                                         0000459503025011  
       IF SIST_LIB = 501 THEN                                           0000459603026011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000459703027011  
          IND := SEC + I_I;                                             0000459803028011  
          TNUMABO[IND] := * + NUMABO[I_I];                              0000459903029011  
          TIMPABO[IND] := * + IMPABO[I_I]*100;                          0000460003030011  
       END                                                              0000460103031011  
       ELSE                                                             0000460203032011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000460303033011  
          IND := SEC + I_I;                                             0000460403034011  
          TNUMCAR[IND] := * + NUMCAR[I_I];                              0000460503035011  
          TIMPCAR[IND] := * + IMPCAR[I_I]*100;                          0000460603036011  
          TNUMABO[IND] := * + NUMABO[I_I];                              0000460703037011  
          TIMPABO[IND] := * + IMPABO[I_I]*100;                          0000460803038011  
       END;                                                             0000460903039011  
 END OF GET_TOTAL;                                                      0000461003040011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000461103041011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_FIRST_TOTAL;                                    0000461203042011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000461303043011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000461403044011

       RSLT_TOT := 7                                                    0000461703047011  
    ELSE                                                                0000461803048011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000461903049011  
    CASE NUMTOT OF                                                      0000462003050011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000462103051011  
       0:                                                               0000462203052011  
         FIND FIRST B04SXCSISUCCAJ AT B04-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND     0000462303053011  
                                      B04-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND     0000462403054011  
                                      B04-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND     0000462503055011  
                                      B04-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND     0000462603056011  
                                      B04-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;    0000462703057011  
       1:                                                               0000462803058011  
         FIND FIRST B14SXCSISUCCAJ AT B14-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND     0000462903059011  
                                      B14-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND     0000463003060011  
                                      B14-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND     0000463103061011  
                                      B14-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND     0000463203062011  
                                      B14-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;    0000463303063011  
       2:                                                               0000463403064011  
         FIND FIRST B24SXCSISUCCAJ AT B24-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND     0000463503065011  
                                      B24-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND     0000463603066011
