                                      B24-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND     0000463803068011  
                                      B24-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;    0000463903069011  
       3:                                                               0000464003070011  
         FIND FIRST B34SXCSISUCCAJ AT B34-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND     0000464103071011  
                                      B34-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND     0000464203072011  
                                      B34-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND     0000464303073011  
                                      B34-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND     0000464403074011  
                                      B34-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;    0000464503075011  
       4:                                                               0000464603076011  
         FIND FIRST B44SXCSISUCCAJ AT B44-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND     0000464703077011  
                                      B44-MOV-CAJINI = ICAJTOT  AND     0000464803078011  
                                      B44-MOV-MONEDA = IMONTOT  AND     0000464903079011  
                                      B44-MOV-BANCOS = INT_BCOS AND     0000465003080011  
                                      B44-MOV-MDA    = MDA :DMERR  ;    0000465103081011  
    ELSE:RSLT_TOT := 5;                                                 0000465203082011  
    END;                                                                0000465303083011  
                                                                        0000465403084011  
    IF BOOLEAN(DMERR)THEN                                               0000465503085011  
       IF DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                                 0000465603086011  
          RSLT_TOT := 1                                                 0000465703087011  
       ELSE                                                             0000465803088011  
          ERRORDB                                                       0000465903089011  
    ELSE                                                                0000466003090011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000466103091011  
       GET_TOTAL;                                                       0000466203092011  
       WHILE FIND_NEXT_TOTAL = 0 DO                                     0000466303093011  
             GET_TOTAL;                                                 0000466403094011  
    END;                                                                0000466503095011  
    END;                                                                0000466603096011  
    FIND_FIRST_TOTAL := RSLT_TOT;                                       0000466703097011  
                                                                        0000466803098011  
 END OF FIND_FIRST_TOTAL;                                               0000466903099011

%********************************************************************   0000467103101011  
%  OUTER BLOCK OF TOTXCAJA                                              0000467203102011  
%********************************************************************   0000467303103011  
 ACQUIREL(E3,A3);                                                       0000467403104011  
 SIZETOT := SIZE(DATOS);                                                0000467503105011  
 REPLACE POINTER(TOTDATOS) BY DATOS FOR SIZETOT;                        0000467603106011  
 RESET(CONXCAJA);                                                       0000467703107011  
 FECTOT:= IFECTOT;                                                      0000467803108011  
 J := -1;                                                               0000467903109011  
 BOOL_J:= FALSE;                                                        0000468003110011  
 WHILE J := * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_J DO                               0000468103111011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000468203112011  
    IF FEC_DIA[J] = IFECTOT THEN                                        0000468303113011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000468403114011  
       IF J > 4 THEN J := * - 5 ;                                       0000468503115011  
       NUMTOT := J;                                                     0000468603116011  
       BOOL_J := TRUE;                                                  0000468703117011  
    END;                                                                0000468803118011  
 END;                                                                   0000468903119011  
 REPLACE STR_BANCOS BY 0        FOR 2 DIGITS,                           0000469003119021  
                       IBORITOT FOR 3 DIGITS,                           0000469103119031  
                       0        FOR 2 DIGITS,                           0000469203119041  
                       IBDESTOT FOR 3 DIGITS;                           0000469303119051  
 INT_BCOS := INTEGER(STR_BANCOS,10);                                    0000469403119061  
                                                                        0000469503119071  
  IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                      0000469603121011  
     ERROR := 7;                                                        0000469703122011  
 IF NOT BOOL_J THEN ERROR := 4;                                         0000469803123011  
                                                                        0000469903124011  
 IF ERROR = 0 THEN                                                      0000470003125011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000470103126011  
    IF SIST_LIB = 500 OR SIST_LIB = 501 THEN                            0000470203127011  
       MDA := 1                                                         0000470303128011  
    ELSE                                                                0000470403129011  
    IF SIST_LIB = 18 THEN                                               0000470503130011  
       MDA := 4                                                         0000470603131011  
    ELSE                                                                0000470703132011  
       MDA := 0;                                                        0000470803133011  
    J := -1;                                                            0000470903134011  
    WHILE J:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                               0000471003135011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000471103136011  
       TNUMCAR[J] := 0;                                                 0000471203137011  
       TIMPCAR[J] := 0;                                                 0000471303138011  
       TNUMABO[J] := 0;                                                 0000471403139011  
       TIMPABO[J] := 0;                                                 0000471503140011  
    END;                                                                0000471603141011  
    IF FIND_FIRST_TOTAL = 0 THEN                                        0000471703142011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000471803143011  
       J := -1;                                                         0000471903144011  
       WHILE J:=*+1 LEQ 19 DO                                           0000472003145011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000472103146011  
                                                                        0000472203147011  
          REPLACE DNCARTOT(J) BY  TNUMCAR[J] FOR  6 DIGITS;             0000472303148011  
          REPLACE DICARTOT(J) BY (TIMPCAR[J] DIV  1000000000)           0000472403149011  
                                          MOD  1000000000 FOR 9 DIGITS, 0000472503150011  
                                  TIMPCAR[J] FOR   9 DIGITS;            0000472603151011  
                                                                        0000472703152011  
          REPLACE DNABOTOT(J) BY  TNUMABO[J] FOR  6 DIGITS;             0000472803153011  
          REPLACE DIABOTOT(J) BY (TIMPABO[J] DIV  1000000000)           0000472903154011  
                                          MOD  1000000000 FOR 9 DIGITS, 0000473003155011  
                                  TIMPABO[J] FOR   9 DIGITS;            0000473103156011  
                                                                        0000473203157011  
       END;                                                             0000473303158011  
    END                                                                 0000473403159011  
 END;                                                                   0000473503160011  
                                                                        0000473603161011  
 TOTXCAJA := ERROR;                                                     0000473703162011  
                                                                        0000473803163011  
   REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(TOTDATOS) FOR SIZETOT;                      0000473903164011  
 CAUSE(CONXCAJA);                                                       0000474003165011  
 END OF TOTXCAJA;                                                       0000474103166011  
%==================================================================     0000474203167011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE TOTXSUC(DATOS);                                      0000474303168011  
 EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                 0000474403169011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000474503170011  
%******************************************************************     0000474603171011  
 INTEGER SUCTOT,CAJATOT,J,FECTOT,NUMTOT,SIZETOT, MONEDA, ERROR;         0000474703172011  
 BOOLEAN BOOL_J;                                                        0000474803173011  
 ARRAY TOTDATOS[0:290];                                                 0000474903174011  
 INTEGER NUM_CAR,  NUM_ABO;                                             0000475003175011  
 DOUBLE  TNUM_CAR, TNUM_ABO, TIMP_CAR, TIMP_ABO, IMP_CAR,  IMP_ABO;     0000475103176011  
                                                                        0000475203177011  
 DEFINE                                                                 0000475303178011  
     IFUNTOT     = INTEGER (POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)                #, % 2   0000475403179011  
     IFECTOT     = INTEGER((POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+  2)      , 08)#, % 8   0000475503180011  
     DFECTOT     =          POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+  2            #, %     0000475603181011  
     ISISTOT     = INTEGER((POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+ 10)      , 04)#, % 4   0000475703182011  
     DSISTOT     =          POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+ 10            #, %     0000475803183011  
     ISUCTOT     = INTEGER((POINTER(TOTDATOS,4)+ 14)      , 04)#, % 4   0000475903184011

    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000478303208011  
       RSLT_NEXT := 7                                                   0000478403209011  
    ELSE                                                                0000478503210011  
    CASE NUMTOT OF                                                      0000478603211011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000478703212011  
       0: FIND NEXT B04SXCSISUCCAJ AT B04-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT AND      0000478803213011  
                                      B04-MOV-MONEDA = MONEDA  : DMERR; 0000478903214011

%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000491903344011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_FIRST_TOTAL;                                    0000492003345011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000492103346011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000492203347011  
 INTEGER RSLT_TOT;                                                      0000492303348011  
    RSLT_TOT := 0 ;                                                     0000492403349011  
    TNUM_CAR := 0 ;                                                     0000492503350011  
    TIMP_CAR := 0 ;                                                     0000492603351011  
    TNUM_ABO := 0 ;                                                     0000492703352011  
    TIMP_ABO := 0 ;                                                     0000492803353011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000492903354011  
       RSLT_TOT := 7                                                    0000493003355011  
    ELSE                                                                0000493103356011  
    CASE NUMTOT OF                                                      0000493203357011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000493303358011  
       0:FIND FIRST B04SXCSISUCCAJ AT B04-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT AND      0000493403359011  
                                      B04-MOV-MONEDA > MONEDA : DMERR;  0000493503360011  
                                                                        0000493603361011  
       1:FIND FIRST B14SXCSISUCCAJ AT B14-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT AND      0000493703362011  
                                      B14-MOV-MONEDA > MONEDA : DMERR;  0000493803363011  
                                                                        0000493903364011  
       2:FIND FIRST B24SXCSISUCCAJ AT B24-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT AND      0000494003365011  
                                      B24-MOV-MONEDA > MONEDA : DMERR;  0000494103366011  
                                                                        0000494203367011  
       3:FIND FIRST B34SXCSISUCCAJ AT B34-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT AND      0000494303368011  
                                      B34-MOV-MONEDA > MONEDA : DMERR;  0000494403369011  
                                                                        0000494503370011  
       4:FIND FIRST B44SXCSISUCCAJ AT B44-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT AND      0000494603371011  
                                      B44-MOV-MONEDA > MONEDA : DMERR;  0000494703372011  
                                                                        0000494803373011  
    ELSE:RSLT_TOT := 5;                                                 0000494903374011  
    END;                                                                0000495003375011  
                                                                        0000495103376011  
    IF BOOLEAN(DMERR)THEN                                               0000495203377011  
       IF DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                                 0000495303378011  
          RSLT_TOT := 1                                                 0000495403379011  
       ELSE                                                             0000495503380011  
          ERRORDB                                                       0000495603381011  
    ELSE                                                                0000495703382011  
    CASE NUMTOT OF                                                      0000495803383011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000495903384011  
       0: GET S151B04CSISUCCAJ(MONEDA := B04-MOV-MONEDA);               0000496003385011  
       1: GET S151B14CSISUCCAJ(MONEDA := B14-MOV-MONEDA);               0000496103386011  
       2: GET S151B24CSISUCCAJ(MONEDA := B24-MOV-MONEDA);               0000496203387011  
       3: GET S151B34CSISUCCAJ(MONEDA := B34-MOV-MONEDA);               0000496303388011  
       4: GET S151B44CSISUCCAJ(MONEDA := B44-MOV-MONEDA);               0000496403389011  
    END;                                                                0000496503390011  
    FIND_FIRST_TOTAL := RSLT_TOT;                                       0000496603391011  
                                                                        0000496703392011  
 END OF FIND_FIRST_TOTAL;                                               0000496803393011  
                                                                        0000496903394011  
%********************************************************************   0000497003395011  
%  OUTER BLOCK OF TOTXSUC                                               0000497103396011  
%********************************************************************   0000497203397011  
 ACQUIREL(E7,A7);                                                       0000497303398011  
 SIZETOT := SIZE(DATOS);                                                0000497403399011  
 REPLACE POINTER(TOTDATOS) BY DATOS FOR SIZETOT;                        0000497503400011  
 RESET(CONXSUC);                                                        0000497603401011  
 FECTOT:= IFECTOT;                                                      0000497703402011  
 MONEDA := IMONCON;                                                     0000497803403011  
 J := -1;                                                               0000497903404011  
 BOOL_J:= FALSE;                                                        0000498003405011  
 WHILE J := * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_J DO                               0000498103406011  
   IF FEC_DIA[J] = IFECTOT THEN                                         0000498203407011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000498303408011  
      IF J > 4 THEN J := * - 5 ;                                        0000498403409011  
      NUMTOT := J;                                                      0000498503410011  
      BOOL_J := TRUE;                                                   0000498603411011  
   END;                                                                 0000498703412011  
                                                                        0000498803413011  
 IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN ERROR := 7;                           0000498903414011  
                                                                        0000499003415011  
 IF NOT BOOL_J THEN ERROR := 4;                                         0000499103416011  
                                                                        0000499203417011  
 IF ERROR = 0 THEN                                                      0000499303418011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000499403419011  
    J := 0;                                                             0000499503420011  
    WHILE FIND_FIRST_TOTAL = 0 DO                                       0000499603421011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000499703422011  
       GET_TOTAL;                                                       0000499803423011  
       WHILE FIND_NEXT_TOTAL = 0 DO GET_TOTAL;                          0000499903424011  
       TIMP_CAR := TIMP_CAR * 100;                                      0000500003425011  
       TIMP_ABO := TIMP_ABO * 100;                                      0000500103426011  
       REPLACE DNCARTOT(J) BY  TNUM_CAR FOR  6 DIGITS;                  0000500203427011  
       REPLACE DICARTOT(J) BY (TIMP_CAR DIV  1000000000)                0000500303428011  
                                        MOD  1000000000 FOR 9 DIGITS,   0000500403429011  
                               TIMP_CAR FOR  9 DIGITS;                  0000500503430011  
                                                                        0000500603431011  
       REPLACE DNABOTOT(J) BY  TNUM_ABO FOR  6 DIGITS;                  0000500703432011  
       REPLACE DIABOTOT(J) BY (TIMP_ABO DIV  1000000000)                0000500803433011  
                                        MOD  1000000000 FOR 9 DIGITS,   0000500903434011  
                               TIMP_ABO FOR  9 DIGITS;                  0000501003435011  
       REPLACE DMON_CSI(J) BY MONEDA    FOR  4 DIGITS;                  0000501103436011  
       J := J + 1;                                                      0000501203437011  
    END                                                                 0000501303438011  
    REPLACE DMONCON BY MONEDA FOR 4 DIGITS;                             0000501403439011  
 END;                                                                   0000501503440011  
                                                                        0000501603441011  
 TOTXSUC := ERROR;                                                      0000501703442011  
                                                                        0000501803443011  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(TOTDATOS) FOR SIZETOT;                        0000501903444011  
 CAUSE(CONXSUC);                                                        0000502003445011  
 END OF TOTXSUC;                                                        0000502103446011

 INTEGER PROCEDURE VALIDACVE ;                                          0000507903502011  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000508003503011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000508103504011  
   NAT_CONTA:= IADFCVECOL(CVETRA) ;                                     0000508203505011  
   IF (( CARGOS AND  NAT_CONTA = 1 )  OR                                0000508303506011  
       ( ABONOS AND  NAT_CONTA = 2 )) AND                               0000508403507011  
        ( MONANT = IDMONEDA) THEN                                       0000508503508011  
      VALIDACVE := 1                                                    0000508603509011  
   ELSE                                                                 0000508703510011  
     VALIDACVE := 0;                                                    0000508803511011  
 END VALIDACVE ;                                                        0000508903512011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000509003513011

%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000509203515011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000509303516011  
                                                                        0000509403517011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000509503518011  
       ERR_CI := 7                                                      0000509603519011  
    ELSE                                                                0000509703520011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000509803521011  
 CASE NUMDIA OF                                                         0000509903522011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000510003523011  
     0:FIND FIRST B01BXMOVCAJ AT B01-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000510103524011  
                                 B01-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000510203525011  
                                 B01-MOV-AUTS151  > AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000510303526011

     1:FIND FIRST B11BXMOVCAJ AT B11-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000510503528011  
                                 B11-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000510603529011  
                                 B11-MOV-AUTS151  > AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000510703530011  
                                                                        0000510803531011  
     2:FIND FIRST B21BXMOVCAJ AT B21-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000510903532011  
                                 B21-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000511003533011  
                                 B21-MOV-AUTS151  > AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000511103534011  
                                                                        0000511203535011  
     3:FIND FIRST B31BXMOVCAJ AT B31-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000511303536011  
                                 B31-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000511403537011  
                                 B31-MOV-AUTS151  > AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000511503538011  
                                                                        0000511603539011  
     4:FIND FIRST B41BXMOVCAJ AT B41-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND         0000511703540011  
                                 B41-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  AND         0000511803541011  
                                 B41-MOV-AUTS151  > AUTS151 :DMERR;     0000511903542011  
     ELSE: ERR_CI := 4;                                                 0000512003543011  
 END;                                                                   0000512103544011  
 IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                                 0000512203545011  
    IF DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                                    0000512303546011  
       ERR_CI := 1                                                      0000512403547011  
    ELSE                                                                0000512503548011  
       ERRORDB  ;                                                       0000512603549011  
 END;                                                                   0000512703550011  
 FIND_FIRST_MOVTO := ERR_CI;                                            0000512803551011  
                                                                        0000512903552011  
 END OF FIND_FIRST_MOVTO;                                               0000513003553011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000513103554011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_NEXT_MOVTO;                                     0000513203555011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000513303556011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000513403557011  
                                                                        0000513503558011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000513603559011  
       ERR_CI := 7                                                      0000513703560011  
    ELSE                                                                0000513803561011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000513903562011  
    CASE NUMDIA OF                                                      0000514003563011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000514103564011  
       0:FIND NEXT B01BXMOVCAJ AT B01-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000514203565011  
                                  B01-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000514303566011  
                                                                        0000514403567011  
       1:FIND NEXT B11BXMOVCAJ AT B11-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000514503568011  
                                  B11-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000514603569011  
                                                                        0000514703570011  
       2:FIND NEXT B21BXMOVCAJ AT B21-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000514803571011  
                                  B21-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000514903572011  
                                                                        0000515003573011  
       3:FIND NEXT B31BXMOVCAJ AT B31-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000515103574011  
                                  B31-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000515203575011  
                                                                        0000515303576011  
       4:FIND NEXT B41BXMOVCAJ AT B41-MOV-SUCINI   = SUCANT  AND        0000515403577011  
                                  B41-MOV-CAJINI   = CAJANT  :DMERR;    0000515503578011  
    ELSE:ERR_CI := 4                                                    0000515603579011  
    END;                                                                0000515703580011  
    IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                              0000515803581011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000515903582011  
       SIGUE:=FALSE;                                                    0000516003583011  
       ERR_CI := 5;                                                     0000516103584011  
    END;                                                                0000516203585011  
    END;                                                                0000516303586011  
    IF ERR_CI = 0 THEN NUM_MOVTOS := 0;                                 0000516403587011  
    FIND_NEXT_MOVTO := ERR_CI;                                          0000516503588011  
                                                                        0000516603589011  
 END OF FIND_NEXT_MOVTO;                                                0000516703590011  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000516803591011  
 PROCEDURE REPLACE_DATOS;                                               0000516903592011  
%------------------------------------------------------------------     0000517003593011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000517103594011  
    IMPORTE := IMPORTE*100;                                             0000517203595011  
    REPLACE DCVETRN(INDI) BY CVETRA     FOR   6 DIGITS,                 0000517303595111  
                             IMPORTE    FOR  16 DIGITS,                 0000517403596011  
                             AUT_APLI   FOR   8 DIGITS,                 0000517503597011  
                             REFER      FOR  20 DIGITS,                 0000517603598011  
                             CUENTA     FOR  20 DIGITS,                 0000517703599011  
                             SUCUR      FOR   4 DIGITS,                 0000517803599111  
                             NAT_CONTA  FOR   1 DIGITS;                 0000517903600011  
    NUM_MOVTOS := * + 1;                                                0000518003601011  
    REPLACE DAUTCON     BY AUTS151    FOR 8 DIGITS,                     0000518103602011  
                           NUM_MOVTOS FOR 2  DIGITS;                    0000518203603011  
 END OF REPLACE_DATOS;                                                  0000518303604011

       3:GET S151B31MOVTOS (CUENTA    := B31-MOV-CTO,                   0000522903644011

     IF STA_MOV = 1 THEN                                                0000525503666011  
        IF RLT_GET = 0 THEN                                             0000525603667011  
        ELSE                                                            0000525703668011  
           RLT_GET := 3                                                 0000525803669011

     GET_DATOS:=RLT_GET;                                                0000526203673011  
 END OF GET_DATOS;                                                      0000526303674011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000526403675011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_IMP_ADIC;                                       0000526503676011  
%--------------------------------------------------------------------   0000526603677011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000526703678011  
    INTEGER RSLT_NEXT,IND;                                              0000526803679011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000526903680011  
       RSLT_NEXT := 7                                                   0000527003681011  
    ELSE                                                                0000527103682011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000527203683011  
    CASE NUMDIA OF                                                      0000527303684011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000527403685011  
         0:BEGIN                                                        0000527503686011  
              FIND B02SXIMPADI AT B02-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151  :DMERR;    0000527603687011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000527703688011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000527803689011  
              ELSE                                                      0000527903690011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000528003691011  
                 IND := -1 ;                                            0000528103692011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000528203693011  
                 GET S151B02IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B02-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000528303694011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B02-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000528403695011  
              END;                                                      0000528503696011  
           END;                                                         0000528603697011  
         1:BEGIN                                                        0000528703698011  
              FIND B12SXIMPADI AT B12-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151  :DMERR;    0000528803699011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000528903700011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000529003701011  
              ELSE                                                      0000529103702011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000529203703011  
                 IND := -1 ;                                            0000529303704011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000529403705011  
                 GET S151B12IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B12-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000529503706011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B12-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000529603707011  
              END;                                                      0000529703708011  
           END;                                                         0000529803709011  
         2:BEGIN                                                        0000529903710011  
              FIND B22SXIMPADI AT B22-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151  :DMERR;    0000530003711011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000530103712011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000530203713011  
              ELSE                                                      0000530303714011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000530403715011  
                 IND := -1;                                             0000530503716011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000530603717011  
                 GET S151B22IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B22-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000530703718011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B22-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000530803719011  
              END;                                                      0000530903720011  
           END;                                                         0000531003721011  
         3:BEGIN                                                        0000531103722011  
              FIND B32SXIMPADI AT B32-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151  :DMERR;    0000531203723011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000531303724011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000531403725011  
              ELSE                                                      0000531503726011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000531603727011  
                 IND := -1;                                             0000531703728011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000531803729011  
                 GET S151B32IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B32-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000531903730011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B32-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000532003731011  
              END;                                                      0000532103732011  
           END;                                                         0000532203733011  
         4:BEGIN                                                        0000532303734011  
              FIND B42SXIMPADI AT B42-MOV-AUTS151 = AUTS151  :DMERR;    0000532403735011  
              IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                    0000532503736011  
                 RSLT_NEXT := 1                                         0000532603737011  
              ELSE                                                      0000532703738011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000532803739011  
                 IND := -1;                                             0000532903740011  
                 WHILE IND:= * + 1 LEQ 3 DO                             0000533003741011  
                 GET S151B42IMPADI(CVE_ADIC[IND]:=B42-MOV-CVETRA[IND+1],0000533103742011  
                                   IMP_ADIC[IND]:=B42-MOV-IMP[IND+1]);  0000533203743011  
              END;                                                      0000533303744011  
           END;                                                         0000533403745011  
      ELSE:RSLT_NEXT:=2 ;                                               0000533503746011  
    END;                                                                0000533603747011  
    END;                                                                0000533703748011  
    FIND_IMP_ADIC := RSLT_NEXT;                                         0000533803749011  
 END OF FIND_IMP_ADIC ;                                                 0000533903750011  
                                                                        0000534003751011  
 PROCEDURE CONTINUACION;   % PROCEDIMIENTO PARA LOCALIZAR Y ENVIAR      0000534103752011  
 BEGIN                     % LA CONTINUACION DE INFORMACION             0000534203753011  
    SIGUE := TRUE;                                                      0000534303754011  
    IF FIND_FIRST_MOVTO = 0 THEN                                        0000534403755011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000534503756011  
       NUM_MOVTOS := 0;                                                 0000534603757011  
       WHILE SIGUE AND NUM_MOVTOS LEQ NMOV DO                           0000534703758011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000534803759011  
        IF GET_DATOS = 0 THEN                                           0000534903760011  
        BEGIN                                                           0000535003761011  
%<INICIO-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)        0000535103761012  
         IF SIST_LIB = 500  AND (NUM_PROC = 40 OR                       0000535203761013  
           NUM_PROC = 41 OR NUM_PROC = 42 OR NUM_PROC = 43) THEN        0000535303761014  
         ELSE                                                           0000535403761016  
         BEGIN                                                          0000535503761017  
%<FIN-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)           0000535603761018  
          IF VALIDACVE = 1 THEN NUM_MOVTOS := * + 1;                    0000535703761019  
          IF INDIMP > 1 THEN                                            0000535803763011  
          BEGIN                                                         0000535903764011  
             IF FIND_IMP_ADIC = 0 THEN                                  0000536003765011  
             BEGIN                                                      0000536103766011  
                INDICE := -1;                                           0000536203767011  
                WHILE INDICE := * + 1 LEQ 3    AND                      0000536303768011  
                      NUM_MOVTOS LEQ NMOV      AND                      0000536403769011  
                      CVE_ADIC[INDICE] > 0     DO                       0000536503770011  
                      IF VALIDACVE = 1 THEN NUM_MOVTOS := * + 1;        0000536603771011  
                INDICE := * - 1;                                        0000536703772011  
                NUM_MOVTOS := 0;                                        0000536803773011  
                WHILE INDICE := * + 1 LEQ 3 AND                         0000536903774011  
                      NUM_MOVTOS LEQ NMOV   AND                         0000537003775011  
                      CVE_ADIC[INDICE] > 0  DO                          0000537103776011  
                      IF VALIDACVE = 1 THEN                             0000537203777011  
                      BEGIN                                             0000537303778011  
                         NUM_MOVTOS := * + 1;                           0000537403779011  
                         INDI := * + 1;                                 0000537503780011  
                         REPLACE_DATOS;                                 0000537603781011  
                      END;                                              0000537703782011  
             END         % FIN DE BUSQUEDA DE IMPORTES ADICIONALES      0000537803783011  
             ELSE SIGUE := FALSE;                                       0000537903784011  
          END            % NO EXISTEN IMPORTES ADICIONALES              0000538003785011  
          ELSE SIGUE  := FALSE;  % NO HAY IMPORTES ADICIONALES          0000538103786011  
         END; % FIN DEL  IF BD_NAME                                     0000538203786021  
        END;             % FIN GET DATOS                                0000538303787011  
       END;              % FIN DEL WHILE                                0000538403788011  
    END;                 % FIN FIND FIRST                               0000538503789011  
 END;                    % FIN DE PROCEDIMIENTO                         0000538603790011  
                                                                        0000538703791011  
                                                                        0000538803792011  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%  0000538903793011  
%            OUTER BLOCK DE CONSULTA CANAL INTELIGENTE               %  0000539003794011  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%  0000539103795011  
 ACQUIREL(E6, A6);                                                      0000539203796011  
 SIZEDATOS:= SIZE(DATOS);                                               0000539303797011  
 NMOV_DET := 23;                                                        0000539403798011  
 REPLACE POINTER(ADATOS1) BY DATOS FOR SIZEDATOS;                       0000539503799011  
 IF (IDAUTCON > 0 OR IDNMOV > 0) THEN INICIO_MOVCI := FALSE             0000539603800011  
                                 ELSE INICIO_MOVCI := TRUE;             0000539703801011  
 FECCONS :=IFECAPLI  ;                                                  0000539803802011  
 SUCANT  :=IDSUC     ;                                                  0000539903803011  
 CAJANT  :=IDCAJA    ;                                                  0000540003804011  
 AUTS151 :=IDAUTCON  ;                                                  0000540103805011  
 MONANT  :=IDMONEDA  ;                                                  0000540203807011  
 NMOV    :=IDNMOV    ;                                                  0000540303809011  
 I := -1 ;                                                              0000540403810011  
 B_FECHA := FALSE;                                                      0000540503811011  
 WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 AND NOT B_FECHA DO                                  0000540603812011  
    IF FECCONS = FEC_DIA[I] THEN                                        0000540703813011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000540803814011  
       IF I > 4 THEN I := * - 5 ;                                       0000540903815011  
       NUMDIA := I ;                                                    0000541003816011  
       B_FECHA:= TRUE;                                                  0000541103817011  
    END;                                                                0000541203818011  
 IF NOT B_FECHA THEN                                                    0000541303819011  
    ERR_CI := 4;                                                        0000541403820011  
 CARGOS := TRUE;                                                        0000541503821011  
 ABONOS := TRUE;                                                        0000541603823011  
 IF INICIO_MOVCI THEN                                                   0000541703825011  
    AUTS151 := 0                                                        0000541803826011  
 ELSE                                                                   0000541903827011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000542003828011  
    AUTS151 := * - 1;                                                   0000542103829011  
    CONTINUACION;                                                       0000542203830011  
    ERR_CI := 0;                                                        0000542303831011  
 END;                                                                   0000542403832011  
                                                                        0000542503833011  
  IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                      0000542603834011  
     ERR_CI := 7;                                                       0000542703835011  
 RESET(CONSULTACI);                                                     0000542803836011  
 ERR_CI := FIND_FIRST_MOVTO;                                            0000542903837011  
 IF ERR_CI = 0 THEN                                                     0000543003838011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000543103839011  
     SIGUE:=TRUE;                                                       0000543203840011  
     INDI :=0;                                                          0000543303841011  
     WHILE SIGUE AND INDI LEQ NMOV_DET DO                               0000543403842011  
     BEGIN                                                              0000543503843011  
        IF GET_DATOS = 0 THEN                                           0000543603844011  
        BEGIN                                                           0000543703845011  
%<INICIO-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)        0000543803845012  
         IF SIST_LIB = 500  AND (NUM_PROC = 40 OR                       0000543903845013  
         NUM_PROC = 41 OR NUM_PROC = 42 OR NUM_PROC = 43) THEN          0000544003845014  
         ELSE                                                           0000544103845016  
         BEGIN                                                          0000544203845017  
%<FIN-IBM> FPC FILTRADO DE MOVS TELETON (SIS 500 PROC 40=>43)           0000544303845018  
           IF VALIDACVE = 1 THEN                                        0000544403846011  
           BEGIN                                                        0000544503847011  
              REPLACE_DATOS;                                            0000544603848011  
              INDI := * + 1;                                            0000544703849011  
           END;                                                         0000544803850011  
           IF INDIMP > 1 THEN                                           0000544903851011  
              IF FIND_IMP_ADIC = 0 THEN                                 0000545003852011  
              BEGIN                                                     0000545103853011  
                 INDICE:=-1 ;                                           0000545203854011  
                 WHILE INDICE:=*+1 LEQ 3    AND                         0000545303855011  
                       INDI < NMOV_DET      AND                         0000545403856011  
                       CVE_ADIC[INDICE] > 0 DO                          0000545503857011  
                  BEGIN                                                 0000545603858011  
                    IMPORTE := IMP_ADIC[INDICE];                        0000545703859011  
                    CVETRA  := CVE_ADIC[INDICE];                        0000545803860011  
                    IF VALIDACVE = 1 THEN                               0000545903861011  
                    BEGIN                                               0000546003862011  
                       REPLACE_DATOS;                                   0000546103863011  
                       INDI := * + 1;                                   0000546203864011  
                    END;                                                0000546303865011  
                  END;                                                  0000546403866011  
              END;                                                      0000546503867011  
         END; %FIN DE IF BD_NAME                                        0000546603867021  
        END; %FIN DE GET DATOS                                          0000546703868011  
        IF INDI = NMOV_DET THEN                                         0000546803869011  
           SIGUE := FALSE                                               0000546903870011  
        ELSE                                                            0000547003871011  
           IF FIND_NEXT_MOVTO = 0 THEN                                  0000547103872011  
           ELSE                                                         0000547203873011  
           BEGIN                                                        0000547303874011  
              ERR_CI := 0;                                              0000547403875011  
              SIGUE := FALSE;                                           0000547503876011  
              REPLACE DAUTCON  BY 0 FOR 10 DIGITS;                      0000547603877011  
           END;                                                         0000547703878011  
     END;                                                               0000547803879011  
 END;                                                                   0000547903880011  
 IF INDI = 0 AND ERR_CI = 0 THEN                                        0000548003881011  
    ERR_CI:=2;                                                          0000548103882011  
                                                                        0000548203883011  
 IF ERR_CI = 20 THEN                                                    0000548303884011  
    ERR_CI := 0;                                                        0000548403885011  
                                                                        0000548503886011  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(ADATOS1) FOR SIZEDATOS;                       0000548603887011  
 CONSULTA_CI:= ERR_CI;                                                  0000548703888011  
 CAUSE(CONSULTACI);                                                     0000548803889011  
 END OF CONSULTA_CI;                                                    0000548903890011  
                                                                        0000549003891011  
%==================================================================     0000549103892011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE TOTALESCI(DATOS);                                    0000549203893011  
 EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                 0000549303894011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000549403895011  
 INTEGER FUNCION, FECHACON, NUM_DIA, IND, ERROR_CI, SIZEDATOS,          0000549503896011  
         TOTMOVTOS;                                                     0000549603897011  
 BOOLEAN BOOL_CTL;                                                      0000549703898011

       0:                                                               0000554603910811

       4:                                                               0000557003913211

    ELSE: RSLT_NEXT:= 5;                                                0000557603913811

    IF RSLT_NEXT = 0 THEN                                               0000557903914111  
       IF BOOLEAN(DMERR)THEN                                            0000558003914211  
          IF   DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                            0000558103914311  
             RSLT_NEXT := 1                                             0000558203914411  
          ELSE                                                          0000558303914511  
          BEGIN                                                         0000558403914611  
             RSLT_NEXT:= 2;                                             0000558503914711

 INTEGER PROCEDURE GET_TOTMDE;                                          0000559303915511  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000559403915611  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000559503915711  
 INTEGER RSLT_GETM,I_I;                                                 0000559603915811  
    CASE NUM_DIA OF                                                     0000559703915911  
    BEGIN                                                               0000559803916011  
       0:BEGIN                                                          0000559903916111  
            GET S151B03CSISUCCAJ(NMDE     := B03-MOV-MDE,               0000560003916211  
                                 NUM_CAJA := B03-MOV-CAJINI,            0000560103916311  
                                 NUM_MON  := B03-MOV-MONEDA);           0000560203916411  
            I_I := -1 ;                                                 0000560303916511  
            WHILE I_I := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                 0000560403916611  
            BEGIN                                                       0000560503916711  
               GET S151B03CSISUCCAJ(NUM_CAR:=B03-NUM-CR[I_I+1],         0000560603916811  
                                    NUM_ABO:=B03-NUM-AB[I_I+1],         0000560703916911  
                                    IMPCAR :=B03-IMP-CR[I_I+1],         0000560803917011  
                                    IMPABO :=B03-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);        0000560903917111  
               TOT_CAR := * + NUM_CAR;                                  0000561003917211  
               TOT_ABO := * + NUM_ABO;                                  0000561103917311  
               TOT_IMPCAR := * + ( IMPCAR * 100);                       0000561203917411  
               TOT_IMPABO := * + ( IMPABO * 100);                       0000561303917511  
            END;                                                        0000561403917611  
         END;                                                           0000561503917711  
       1:BEGIN                                                          0000561603917811  
            GET S151B13CSISUCCAJ(NMDE     := B13-MOV-MDE,               0000561703917911  
                                 NUM_CAJA := B13-MOV-CAJINI,            0000561803918011  
                                 NUM_MON  := B13-MOV-MONEDA);           0000561903918111

            BEGIN                                                       0000562203918411

                                    NUM_ABO:=B13-NUM-AB[I_I+1],         0000562403918611

                                    IMPABO :=B13-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);        0000562603918811  
               TOT_CAR := * + NUM_CAR;                                  0000562703918911  
               TOT_ABO := * + NUM_ABO;                                  0000562803919011  
               TOT_IMPCAR := * + ( IMPCAR * 100 );                      0000562903919111  
               TOT_IMPABO := * + ( IMPABO * 100 );                      0000563003919211  
            END;                                                        0000563103919311  
         END;                                                           0000563203919411  
       2:BEGIN                                                          0000563303919511  
            GET S151B23CSISUCCAJ(NMDE     := B23-MOV-MDE,               0000563403919611

                                    NUM_ABO:=B23-NUM-AB[I_I+1],         0000564103920311  
                                    IMPCAR :=B23-IMP-CR[I_I+1],         0000564203920411  
                                    IMPABO :=B23-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);        0000564303920511  
               TOT_CAR := * + NUM_CAR;                                  0000564403920611  
               TOT_ABO := * + NUM_ABO;                                  0000564503920711  
               TOT_IMPCAR := * + ( IMPCAR * 100 );                      0000564603920811  
               TOT_IMPABO := * + ( IMPABO * 100 );                      0000564703920911  
            END;                                                        0000564803921011  
         END;                                                           0000564903921111  
       3:BEGIN                                                          0000565003921211  
            GET S151B33CSISUCCAJ(NMDE     := B33-MOV-MDE,               0000565103921311  
                                 NUM_CAJA := B33-MOV-CAJINI,            0000565203921411  
                                 NUM_MON  := B33-MOV-MONEDA);           0000565303921511  
            I_I := -1 ;                                                 0000565403921611  
            WHILE I_I := * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                 0000565503921711  
            BEGIN                                                       0000565603921811  
               GET S151B33CSISUCCAJ(NUM_CAR:=B33-NUM-CR[I_I+1],         0000565703921911  
                                    NUM_ABO:=B33-NUM-AB[I_I+1],         0000565803922011  
                                    IMPCAR :=B33-IMP-CR[I_I+1],         0000565903922111  
                                    IMPABO :=B33-IMP-AB[I_I+1]);        0000566003922211  
               TOT_CAR := * + NUM_CAR;                                  0000566103922311  
               TOT_ABO := * + NUM_ABO;                                  0000566203922411  
               TOT_IMPCAR := * + ( IMPCAR * 100 );                      0000566303922511  
               TOT_IMPABO := * + ( IMPABO * 100 );                      0000566403922611  
            END;                                                        0000566503922711  
         END;                                                           0000566603922811  
       4:BEGIN                                                          0000566703922911  
            GET S151B43CSISUCCAJ(NMDE     := B43-MOV-MDE,               0000566803923011  
                                 NUM_CAJA := B43-MOV-CAJINI,            0000566903923111  
                                 NUM_MON  := B43-MOV-MONEDA);           0000567003923211

    END;                                                                0000568503924711  
 END OF GET_TOTMDE;                                                     0000568603924811  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000568703924911  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_TOT_SUCCAJ;                                     0000568803925011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000568903925111  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000569003925211  
 INTEGER RSLT_TOT;                                                      0000569103925311  
 IF NOT INICIO_TOTCI THEN                                               0000569203925411  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000569303925511  
   CASE NUM_DIA OF                                                      0000569403925611  
   BEGIN                                                                0000569503925711  
    0:FIND FIRST B03SXCSISUCCAJ AT B03-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND        0000569603925811  
                                   B03-MOV-CAJINI = NUM_CAJA AND        0000569703925911  
                                   B03-MOV-MONEDA > 0        :DMERR ;   0000569803926011  
%                                  B03-MOV-BANCOS = 0        AND        0000569903926111  
%                                  B03-MOV-MDE    = 0        :DMERR ;   0000570003926211  
    1:FIND FIRST B13SXCSISUCCAJ AT B13-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND        0000570103926311  
                                   B13-MOV-CAJINI = NUM_CAJA AND        0000570203926411  
                                   B13-MOV-MONEDA > 0        :DMERR ;   0000570303926511  
%                                  B13-MOV-BANCOS = 0        AND        0000570403926611  
%                                  B13-MOV-MDE    = 0        :DMERR ;   0000570503926711  
    2:FIND FIRST B23SXCSISUCCAJ AT B23-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND        0000570603926811  
                                   B23-MOV-CAJINI = NUM_CAJA AND        0000570703926911  
                                   B23-MOV-MONEDA > 0        :DMERR ;   0000570803927011  
%                                  B23-MOV-BANCOS = 0        AND        0000570903927111  
%                                  B23-MOV-MDE    = 0        :DMERR ;   0000571003927211  
    3:FIND FIRST B33SXCSISUCCAJ AT B33-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND        0000571103927411  
                                   B33-MOV-CAJINI = NUM_CAJA AND        0000571203927511  
                                   B33-MOV-MONEDA > 0        :DMERR ;   0000571303927611  
%                                  B33-MOV-BANCOS = 0        AND        0000571403927711  
%                                  B33-MOV-MDE    = 0        :DMERR ;   0000571503927811  
    4:FIND FIRST B43SXCSISUCCAJ AT B43-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND        0000571603927911  
                                   B43-MOV-CAJINI = NUM_CAJA AND        0000571703928011  
                                   B43-MOV-MONEDA > 0        :DMERR ;   0000571803928111  
%                                  B43-MOV-BANCOS = 0        AND        0000571903928211  
%                                  B43-MOV-MDE    = 0        :DMERR ;   0000572003928311  
    ELSE:RSLT_TOT := 5;                                                 0000572103928411  
    END;                                                                0000572203928511  
 END                                                                    0000572303928611  
 ELSE                                                                   0000572403928711  
    RSLT_TOT := FIND_NEXT_MONEDA;                                       0000572503928811  
                                                                        0000572603928911  
 IF RSLT_TOT = 0 THEN                                                   0000572703929011  
    IF BOOLEAN(DMERR)THEN                                               0000572803929111  
       IF   DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND                                    0000572903929211  
       THEN RSLT_TOT := 1                                               0000573003929311  
       ELSE ERRORDB;                                                    0000573103929411  
                                                                        0000573203929511  
 FIND_TOT_SUCCAJ  := RSLT_TOT;                                          0000573303929611  
END OF FIND_TOT_SUCCAJ;                                                 0000573403929711  
%********************************************************************   0000573503929811  
%  OUTER BLOCK OF TOTALE-SUC-CAJA                                       0000573603929911  
%********************************************************************   0000573703930011  
 NMOV_TOT := 25;                                                        0000573803930111  
 IF IMON_CON = 0                                                        0000573903930211  
 THEN INICIO_TOTCI := TRUE                                              0000574003930311  
 ELSE INICIO_TOTCI := FALSE;                                            0000574103930411  
 NUM_CAJA := ICAJTOT;                                                   0000574203930511  
 NUM_MON  := IMON_CON;                                                  0000574303930611  
 NUM_CAR:= NUM_ABO := TOT_IMPCAR := TOT_IMPABO := 0;                    0000574403930711  
 IF FIND_TOT_SUCCAJ = 0 THEN                                            0000574503930811  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000574603930911  
    INICIO_TOTCI := FALSE;                                              0000574703931011  
    GET_TOTMDE;                                                         0000574803931111  
    RESULT_NEXT := 0;                                                   0000574903931211  
    IND_MDE := 0;                                                       0000575003931311  
    WHILE RESULT_NEXT = 0 AND IND_MDE < NMOV_TOT DO                     0000575103931411  
    BEGIN                                                               0000575203931511  
       REPLACE DMON_CON BY NUM_MON FOR 4 DIGITS;                        0000575303931611  
       REPLACE DMONEDA(IND_MDE)  BY NUM_MON  FOR 4 DIGITS   ,           0000575403931711  
                                    TOT_CAR  FOR 8 DIGITS   ,           0000575503931811  
                                    TOT_IMPCAR FOR 18 DIGITS,           0000575603931911  
                                    TOT_ABO  FOR 8 DIGITS   ,           0000575703932011  
                                    TOT_IMPABO FOR 18 DIGITS,           0000575803932111  
                                    0          FOR 4 DIGITS   ;         0000575903932211  
       IND_MDE := * + 1;                                                0000576003932311  
       RESULT_NEXT := FIND_NEXT_MONEDA;                                 0000576103932411  
       TOT_CAR := TOT_ABO := TOT_IMPCAR := TOT_IMPABO := 0;             0000576203932511  
       IF RESULT_NEXT = 0 THEN     % SI EL ERROR ES 1 EN FIND NEXT      0000576303932611  
          GET_TOTMDE;              % SI NO HAY ERROR LEE LOS DATOS      0000576403933111  
    END;                                                                0000576503933211  
 END;                                                                   0000576603933311  
 IF (IND_MDE < NMOV_TOT) OR (RESULT_NEXT > 0) THEN                      0000576703933411  
       REPLACE DMON_CON BY 0 FOR 4 DIGITS;                              0000576803933511  
                                                                        0000576903933611  
 TOTCAJ_CI := 0;                                                        0000577003933711  
 END OF TOTXSUCCAJ;                                                     0000577103933811

%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000580404041751

%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000580604041811  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000580704041841

 IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN RSLT_NEXT := 7                        0000580904041901

 BEGIN                                                                  0000581104041961  
    CASE NUM_DIA OF                                                     0000581204041991  
    BEGIN                                                               0000581304042021  
       0: BEGIN                                                         0000581404042051  
             FIND NEXT  B03SXCSISUCCAJ AT B03-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  AND 0000581504042081  
                                          B03-MOV-CAJINI = NUM_CAJA AND 0000581604042111  
                                          B03-MOV-MONEDA = NUM_MON  AND 0000581704042141  
                                          B03-MOV-BANCOS = NUM_BCO  AND 0000581804042171  
                                          B03-MOV-MDE    > NMDE :DMERR; 0000581904042201  
             IF BOOLEAN(DMERR)THEN                                      0000582004042231  
             BEGIN                                                      0000582104042261
