%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000610704048981  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FIND_TOT_SUC;                                        0000610804049011  
%-------------------------------------------------------------------    0000610904049041  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000611004049071  
 INTEGER RSLT_TOT;                                                      0000611104049101  
 IF INICIO_TOTCI THEN                                                   0000611204049131  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000611304049161  
   CASE NUM_DIA OF                                                      0000611404049191  
   BEGIN                                                                0000611504049221  
    0:FIND FIRST B03SXCSISUCCAJ AT B03-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  :DMERR;    0000611604049251  
    1:FIND FIRST B13SXCSISUCCAJ AT B13-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  :DMERR;    0000611704049401  
    2:FIND FIRST B23SXCSISUCCAJ AT B23-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  :DMERR;    0000611804049551  
    3:FIND FIRST B33SXCSISUCCAJ AT B33-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  :DMERR;    0000611904049701  
    4:FIND FIRST B43SXCSISUCCAJ AT B43-MOV-SUCINI = ISUCTOT  :DMERR;    0000612004049851  
    ELSE:RSLT_TOT := 5;                                                 0000612104050001  
    END;                                                                0000612204050031  
 END                                                                    0000612304050061  
 ELSE                                                                   0000612404050091  
    RSLT_TOT := FIND_NEXT_MONEDA;                                       0000612504050121  
 IF RSLT_TOT = 0 THEN                                                   0000612604050181  
    IF BOOLEAN(DMERR)THEN                                               0000612704050211  
       IF   DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND                                    0000612804050241  
       THEN RSLT_TOT := 1                                               0000612904050271  
       ELSE ERRORDB;                                                    0000613004050301  
                                                                        0000613104050331  
 FIND_TOT_SUC := RSLT_TOT;                                              0000613204050361  
END OF FIND_TOT_SUC;                                                    0000613304050391  
                                                                        0000613404050421  
%********************************************************************   0000613504050451  
%  OUTER BLOCK OF TOTALE-SUC                                            0000613604050481  
%********************************************************************   0000613704050511  
 NMOV_TOT := 25;                                                        0000613804050541  
 NUM_CAJA := ICAJ_CON;                                                  0000613904050543  
 NUM_MON  := IMON_CON;                                                  0000614004050545  
 NUM_BCO  := NMDE := 0;                                                 0000614104050551  
                                                                        0000614204050561  
 IF NUM_CAJA = 0 AND NUM_MON = 0                                        0000614304050571  
 THEN INICIO_TOTCI := TRUE                                              0000614404050601  
 ELSE                                                                   0000614504050611  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000614604050621  
    INICIO_TOTCI := FALSE;                                              0000614704050631  
    NUM_MON      := * + 1;                                              0000614804050641  
 END;                                                                   0000614904050651  
                                                                        0000615004050661  
 CORTE_CAJA := CORTE_MONEDA := FALSE;                                   0000615104050671  
 NUM_CAR  := NUM_ABO := TOT_IMPCAR := TOT_IMPABO := 0;                  0000615204050721  
 TOTSUC_CAR := TOTSUC_ABO := TOTSUC_IMPCAR := TOTSUC_IMPABO := 0;       0000615304050731  
 IF FIND_TOT_SUC  = 0 THEN                                              0000615404050751  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000615504050781  
    INICIO_TOTCI := FALSE;                                              0000615604050811  
    RESULT_NEXT := 0;                                                   0000615704050871  
    IND_MDE := -1;                                                      0000615804050901  
    WHILE RESULT_NEXT = 0 AND IND_MDE < NMOV_TOT DO                     0000615904050931  
    BEGIN                                                               0000616004050961  
       IND_MDE := * + 1;                                                0000616104051231  
       CORTE_CAJA := CORTE_MONEDA := FALSE;                             0000616204051241  
       WHILE NOT (CORTE_CAJA    OR                                      0000616304051251  
                  CORTE_MONEDA) AND                                     0000616404051261  
                  RESULT_NEXT = 0  DO                                   0000616504051271  
       BEGIN                                                            0000616604051281  
           GET_TOTSUC;                                                  0000616704051291  
           RESULT_NEXT := FIND_NEXT_MONEDA;                             0000616804051301  
           IF CORTE_CAJA OR CORTE_MONEDA THEN                           0000616904051311  
           BEGIN                                                        0000617004051321  
              TOTSUC_CAR    := * + TOT_CAR;                             0000617104051331  
              TOTSUC_ABO    := * + TOT_ABO;                             0000617204051333  
              TOTSUC_IMPCAR := * + TOT_IMPCAR;                          0000617304051335  
              TOTSUC_IMPABO := * + TOT_IMPABO;                          0000617404051341  
              TOT_CAR := TOT_ABO := TOT_IMPCAR := TOT_IMPABO := 0;      0000617504051355  
              CORTE_CAJA := CORTE_MONEDA := FALSE;                      0000617604051357  
           END;                                                         0000617704051359  
       END;                                                             0000617804051361  
    END;                                                                0000617904051501  
 END;                                                                   0000618004051531  
 IF IND_MDE < NMOV_TOT THEN                                             0000618104051561  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000618204051591  
    REPLACE DCAJ_CON BY NUM_CAJA FOR 4 DIGITS,                          0000618304051595  
                        NUM_MON FOR 4 DIGITS;                           0000618404051597  
    REPLACE DCAJA(IND_MDE)  BY   NUM_CAJA FOR 4 DIGITS   ,              0000618504051599  
                                 NUM_MON  FOR 4 DIGITS   ,              0000618604051601  
                                 TOTSUC_CAR  FOR 8 DIGITS   ,           0000618704051603  
                                 TOTSUC_IMPCAR FOR 18 DIGITS,           0000618804051605  
                                 TOTSUC_ABO  FOR 8 DIGITS   ,           0000618904051607  
                                 TOTSUC_IMPABO FOR 18 DIGITS;           0000619004051609  
 END;                                                                   0000619104051641  
 IF RESULT_NEXT >  0 THEN REPLACE DCAJ_CON BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;           0000619204051643  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(TOTDATOS) FOR SIZEDATOS;                      0000619304051646  
 TOTSUC_CI := 0;                                                        0000619404051651  
 END OF TOTSUC;                                                         0000619504051681  
                                                                        0000619604185011  
                                                                        0000619704186011  
%********************************************************************   0000619804187011  
%  OUTER BLOCK OF TOTALESCI  ( TOTALES CANAL INTELIGENTE )              0000619904188011  
%********************************************************************   0000620004189011  
 ACQUIREL(E8 ,A8);                                                      0000620104190011  
 RESET(CONXSUCMDE);                                                     0000620204191011  
 SIZEDATOS := SIZE(DATOS);                                              0000620304192011  
 REPLACE POINTER(TOTDATOS) BY DATOS FOR SIZEDATOS;                      0000620404193011  
 FECHACON := IFECCON;                                                   0000620504194011  
 FUNCION  := IFUNCON;                                                   0000620604195011  
 IND := -1;                                                             0000620704196011  
 BOOL_CTL:= FALSE;                                                      0000620804197011  
 WHILE IND := * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_CTL DO                           0000620904198011  
   IF FEC_DIA[IND] = FECHACON THEN                                      0000621004199011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000621104200011  
      IF IND > 4 THEN IND := * - 5;                                     0000621204201011  
      NUM_DIA := IND;                                                   0000621304202011  
      BOOL_CTL := TRUE;                                                 0000621404203011  
   END;                                                                 0000621504204011  
 IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN ERROR_CI := 7;                        0000621604206011  
 IF NOT BOOL_CTL THEN ERROR_CI := 4;                                    0000621704207011  
 IF ERROR_CI = 0 THEN                                                   0000621804208011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000621904209011  
    CASE FUNCION OF                                                     0000622004210011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000622104211011  
       1: ERROR_CI := TOTSUC_CI; % FUNCION NO ACTIVA                    0000622204212011  
       2: ERROR_CI := TOTCAJ_CI; % TOTAL POR SUCURSAL/CAJA              0000622304212111  
       ELSE : ERROR_CI := 3;                                            0000622404213011  
    END;                                                                0000622504214011  
 END;                                                                   0000622604215011  
 TOTALESCI := ERROR_CI;                                                 0000622704216011  
 REPLACE DATOS BY POINTER(TOTDATOS) FOR SIZEDATOS;                      0000622804217011  
 CAUSE(CONXSUCMDE);                                                     0000622904218011  
 END OF TOTALESCI;                                                      0000623004219011  
                                                                        0000623104220011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE MOVESPECIFICO(DATOS);                                0000623204221011  
 EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                 0000623304222011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000623404223011  
  INTEGER IN_FECHA, IN_PROD, IN_SUC, IN_CAJA, IN_MONEDA, IN_AUTAPL,     0000623504224011  
          BUSCA, BD_AUTAPL, BD_MONEDA, ERR_FIND, NUM_DATA, ERR_ESP,     0000623604225011  
          STA_MOV, INDICE, LONG_DATOS;                                  0000623704226011  
  ARRAY   DATOS_ENT[0:34];                                              0000623804227011  
  BOOLEAN SALIDA;                                                       0000623904228011  
  DEFINE                                                                0000624004229011  
     ID_FECHA    = INTEGER(POINTER(DATOS_ENT)     ,08)   #,             0000624104230011  
     ID_PRODUCTO = INTEGER(POINTER(DATOS_ENT)+ 08 ,04)   #,             0000624204231011  
     ID_SUC      = INTEGER(POINTER(DATOS_ENT)+ 12 ,04)   #,             0000624304232011  
     ID_CAJA     = INTEGER(POINTER(DATOS_ENT)+ 16 ,02)   #,             0000624404233011  
     ID_MONEDA   = INTEGER(POINTER(DATOS_ENT)+ 18 ,04)   #,             0000624504234011  
     ID_AUTAPL   = INTEGER(POINTER(DATOS_ENT)+ 22 ,08)   #;             0000624604235011  
                                                                        0000624704236011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FF_SUC_CAJ;                                          0000624804237011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000624904238011  
    ERR_ESP := 0;                                                       0000625004239011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000625104240011  
       ERR_ESP := 7               % BASE DE DATOS NO DISPONIBLE         0000625204241011  
    ELSE                                                                0000625304242011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000625404243011  
       CASE NUM_DATA OF                                                 0000625504244011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000625604245011  
          0:FIND FIRST B01BXMOVCAJ AT B01-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND      0000625704246011  
                                      B01-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;  0000625804247011  
                                                                        0000625904248011  
          1:FIND FIRST B11BXMOVCAJ AT B11-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND      0000626004249011  
                                      B11-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;  0000626104250011  
                                                                        0000626204251011  
          2:FIND FIRST B21BXMOVCAJ AT B21-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND      0000626304252011  
                                      B21-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;  0000626404253011  
                                                                        0000626504254011  
          3:FIND FIRST B31BXMOVCAJ AT B31-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND      0000626604255011  
                                      B31-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;  0000626704256011  
                                                                        0000626804257011  
          4:FIND FIRST B41BXMOVCAJ AT B41-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND      0000626904258011  
                                      B41-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;  0000627004259011  
          ELSE:  ERR_ESP := 5 ;   % ERROR EN NUMERO DE DATASET          0000627104260011  
       END;                                                             0000627204261011  
       IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                           0000627304262011  
          IF DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                              0000627404263011  
             ERR_ESP := 1         % NO EXISTE INFORMACION               0000627504264011  
          ELSE                                                          0000627604265011  
          BEGIN                                                         0000627704266011  
             ERRORDB  ;                                                 0000627804267011  
             ERR_ESP := 9;        % ERROR EN BASE DE DATOS              0000627904268011  
          END;                                                          0000628004269011  
    END;                                                                0000628104270011  
    FF_SUC_CAJ := ERR_ESP;                                              0000628204271011  
 END OF FF_SUC_CAJ;                                                     0000628304272011  
                                                                        0000628404273011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE FN_SUC_CAJ;                                          0000628504274011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000628604275011  
    ERR_ESP := 0;                                                       0000628704276011  
    IF NOT HAPPENED (BASE_OPEN) THEN                                    0000628804277011  
       ERR_ESP := 7                % BASE DE DATOS NO DISPONIBLE        0000628904278011  
    ELSE                                                                0000629004279011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000629104280011  
       CASE NUM_DATA OF                                                 0000629204281011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000629304282011  
          0:FIND NEXT B01BXMOVCAJ AT B01-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND       0000629404283011  
                                     B01-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;   0000629504284011  
                                                                        0000629604285011  
          1:FIND NEXT B11BXMOVCAJ AT B11-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND       0000629704286011  
                                     B11-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;   0000629804287011  
                                                                        0000629904288011  
          2:FIND NEXT B21BXMOVCAJ AT B21-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND       0000630004289011  
                                     B21-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;   0000630104290011  
                                                                        0000630204291011  
          3:FIND NEXT B31BXMOVCAJ AT B31-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND       0000630304292011  
                                     B31-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;   0000630404293011  
                                                                        0000630504294011  
          4:FIND NEXT B41BXMOVCAJ AT B41-MOV-SUCINI = IN_SUC  AND       0000630604295011  
                                     B41-MOV-CAJINI = IN_CAJA :DMERR;   0000630704296011  
          ELSE:  ERR_ESP := 5 ;    % ERROR EN NUNERO DE DATASET         0000630804297011  
       END;                                                             0000630904298011  
       IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                           0000631004299011  
          IF DMERR.DMERROR = NOTFOUND THEN                              0000631104300011  
             ERR_ESP := 1          % ERROR NO EXISTE INFORMACION        0000631204301011  
          ELSE                                                          0000631304302011  
          BEGIN                                                         0000631404303011  
             ERRORDB  ;                                                 0000631504304011  
             ERR_ESP := 9;         % ERROR EN BASE DE DATOS             0000631604305011  
          END;                                                          0000631704306011  
    END;                                                                0000631804307011  
    FN_SUC_CAJ  := ERR_ESP;                                             0000631904308011  
 END OF FN_SUC_CAJ;                                                     0000632004309011  
                                                                        0000632104310011  
 INTEGER PROCEDURE TOMA_DATOS;                                          0000632204311011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000632304312011  
    INTEGER RESLT_GET;                                                  0000632404313011  
    RESLT_GET := 0;                                                     0000632504314011  
    CASE NUM_DATA OF                                                    0000632604315011  
    BEGIN                                                               0000632704316011  
       0:GET S151B01MOVTOS (BD_AUTAPL  := B01-MOV-AUTAPL,               0000632804317011  
                            BD_MONEDA  := B01-MOV-MONEDA,               0000632904318011  
                            STA_MOV    := B01-MOV-STA   );              0000633004319011  
       1:GET S151B11MOVTOS (BD_AUTAPL  := B11-MOV-AUTAPL,               0000633104320011  
                            BD_MONEDA  := B11-MOV-MONEDA,               0000633204321011  
                            STA_MOV    := B11-MOV-STA   );              0000633304322011  
       2:GET S151B21MOVTOS (BD_AUTAPL  := B21-MOV-AUTAPL,               0000633404323011  
                            BD_MONEDA  := B21-MOV-MONEDA,               0000633504324011  
                            STA_MOV    := B21-MOV-STA   );              0000633604325011  
       3:GET S151B31MOVTOS (BD_AUTAPL  := B31-MOV-AUTAPL,               0000633704326011  
                            BD_MONEDA  := B31-MOV-MONEDA,               0000633804327011  
                            STA_MOV    := B31-MOV-STA   );              0000633904328011  
       4:GET S151B41MOVTOS (BD_AUTAPL  := B41-MOV-AUTAPL,               0000634004329011  
                            BD_MONEDA  := B41-MOV-MONEDA,               0000634104330011  
                            STA_MOV    := B41-MOV-STA   );              0000634204331011  
       ELSE:RESLT_GET:= 5 ;    % ERROR NUMERO DE DATASET                0000634304332011  
     END;                                                               0000634404333011  
     IF RESLT_GET = 0 THEN     % GET OK                                 0000634504334011  
     ELSE                                                               0000634604335011  
     RESLT_GET := 3;           % ERROR AL OBTENER DATOS DEL REGISTRO    0000634704336011  
                                                                        0000634804337011  
     TOMA_DATOS:=RESLT_GET;                                             0000634904338011  
 END OF TOMA_DATOS;                                                     0000635004339011  
                                                                        0000635104340011  
%BLOCK PROCEDIMIENTO MOVESPECIFICO                                      0000635204341011  
   RESET(CON_MESP);                                                     0000635304342011  
   LONG_DATOS := SIZE(DATOS);                                           0000635404343011  
   REPLACE POINTER(DATOS_ENT) BY DATOS FOR LONG_DATOS;                  0000635504344011  
   IN_FECHA := ID_FECHA;                                                0000635604345011  
   IN_PROD  := ID_PRODUCTO;                                             0000635704346011  
   IN_SUC   := ID_SUC;                                                  0000635804347011  
   IN_CAJA  := ID_CAJA;                                                 0000635904348011  
   IN_MONEDA:= ID_MONEDA;                                               0000636004349011  
   IN_AUTAPL:= ID_AUTAPL;                                               0000636104350011  
   SALIDA   := FALSE;                                                   0000636204351011  
   WHILE INDICE := * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT SALIDA DO                        0000636304352011  
   IF FEC_DIA[INDICE] = IN_FECHA THEN                                   0000636404353011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000636504354011  
      IF INDICE > 4 THEN INDICE := * - 5;                               0000636604355011  
      NUM_DATA := INDICE;                                               0000636704356011  
      SALIDA   := TRUE;                                                 0000636804357011  
   END;                                                                 0000636904358011  
   IF SALIDA THEN                                                       0000637004359011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000637104360011  
      ERR_FIND := FF_SUC_CAJ;                                           0000637204361011  
      IF ERR_FIND = 0 THEN                                              0000637304362011  
      BEGIN                                                             0000637404363011  
         SALIDA   := FALSE;                                             0000637504364011  
         WHILE ERR_FIND = 0 AND NOT SALIDA DO                           0000637604365011  
         BEGIN                                                          0000637704366011  
            ERR_FIND := TOMA_DATOS;                                     0000637804367011  
            IF ERR_FIND = 0 THEN                                        0000637904368011  
               IF BD_AUTAPL = IN_AUTAPL THEN                            0000638004369011  
               BEGIN                                                    0000638104370011  
                  SALIDA := TRUE;                                       0000638204371011  
                  IF STA_MOV   = 1         AND                          0000638304372011  
                     IN_MONEDA = BD_MONEDA THEN                         0000638404373011  
                  ELSE                                                  0000638504374011  
                     ERR_FIND := 2;                                     0000638604375011  
               END                                                      0000638704376011  
               ELSE                                                     0000638804377011  
                  ERR_FIND := FN_SUC_CAJ;                               0000638904378011  
         END;                                                           0000639004379011  
      END;                                                              0000639104380011  
   END                                                                  0000639204381011  
   ELSE ERR_FIND := 4;         % ERROR EN FECHA DE CONSULTA             0000639304382011  
   MOVESPECIFICO := ERR_FIND ;                                          0000639404383011  
   CAUSE(CON_MESP);                                                     0000639504384011  
 END OF MOVESPECIFICO ;                                                 0000639604385011  
                                                                        0000639704386011

              "ERROR EN CTLVERS, NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE ",          0000643504410011  
              "LIBRERIA DE L001/CONTROL ";                              0000643604411011  
      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                  0000643704412011  
   END                                                                  0000643804413011  
   ELSE                                                                 0000643904414011  
      REPLACE CONTROL.TITLE BY LIBRERIA FOR 72;                         0000644004415011  
                                                                        0000644104416011  
   REPLACE CONSULTAS BY "S151L002R" , POINTER(NOM_LIB)+31 FOR 3;        0000644204417011  
                                                                        0000644304418011  
   IF DAME_TIT(CONSULTAS,LIBRERIA) < 0 THEN                             0000644404419011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000644504420011  
      REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                             0000644604421011

              "ERROR EN CTLVERS, NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE ",          0000644804423011  
              "LIBRERIA DE L002/REGISTRA ";                             0000644904424011

             END  ;                                                     0000690104795301  
        20 : BEGIN                                                      0000690204795311

                        "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,    0000690404795331

             END  ;                                                     0000690804795371  
        ELSE :    ;                                                     0000690904795381  
     END;                                                               0000691004795391  
  END;                                                                  0000691104795411

 PROCEDURE CARGAFECHAS;                                                 0000707504800011  
%=============================================================          0000707604801011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000707704802011  
   WHILE IND := LINKLIBRARY(CONTROL) < 0 DO         ;                   0000707804803011  
   REPLACE SISDFUNCI(ACONTROLE1) BY 1 FOR 2 DIGITS,                     0000707904804011  
                                    SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,              0000708004805011  
                                    CSI_LIB  FOR 2 DIGITS;              0000708104806011  
   RESULT:= CONSISDIA(ACONTROLE1);                                      0000708204807011  
   WHILE IND := DELINKLIBRARY(CONTROL) < 0 DO  ;                        0000708304808011  
   IF RESULT > 0 THEN                                                   0000708404809011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000708504810011  
     REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/CONSMOVS",                            0000708604811011  
                       SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                           0000708704812011  
                       ", RESULT L001/CONTROL = ",                      0000708804813011  
                       RESULT FOR 6 DIGITS;                             0000708904814011  
     LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                   0000709004815011  
     MYSELF.STATUS:= -1;                                                0000709104816011  
   END;                                                                 0000709204817011  
                                                                        0000709304818011  
   REPLACE NOM_BDM BY "(S151)",                                         0000709404819011  
                      SISDNOMBDM(ACONTROLE1) FOR 17,                    0000709504820011  
                      " ON CMEMP.";                                     0000709604821011  
   BD_NAME:= STRING(NOM_BDM,45);                                        0000709704822011  
   FECPROC := ISISDFECPRO(ACONTROLE1);                                  0000709804823011  
   REPLACE ARC_MOV BY SISDNOMMOV(ACONTROLE1) FOR 34;                    0000709904824011  
   REPLACE PK_MOV  BY SISDPKMOVS(ACONTROLE1) FOR 17;                    0000710004825011  
   REPLACE ARC_DES BY SISDNOMDES(ACONTROLE1) FOR 34;                    0000710104826011  
   REPLACE PK_DES  BY SISDPKDESS(ACONTROLE1) FOR 17;                    0000710204827011  
   IND := -1;                                                           0000710304828011  
   WHILE IND:=* + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                            0000710404829011  
   IF ISISDFECARM(ACONTROLE1,IND) > 0 THEN                              0000710504830011  
   BEGIN                                                                0000710604831011  
     FEC_DIA[IND] := ISISDFECARM(ACONTROLE1,IND) ;                      0000710704832011  
     REPLACE NOM_ARC BY ARC_MOV FOR 34,                                 0000710804833011  
             FEC_DIA[IND] FOR 6 DIGITS,                                 0000710904834011  
             " ON ", PK_MOV FOR 17, "." ;                               0000711004835011  
     REPLACE MOVS[IND].TITLE BY NOM_ARC;                                0000711104836011  
     REPLACE NOM_ARC BY ARC_DES FOR 34,                                 0000711204837011  
             FEC_DIA[IND] FOR 6 DIGITS,                                 0000711304838011  
             " ON ", PK_DES FOR 17, "." ;                               0000711404839011  
     REPLACE DESS[IND].TITLE BY NOM_ARC;                                0000711504840011  
  END                                                                   0000711604841011  
  ELSE                                                                  0000711704842011  
     FEC_DIA[IND] := 0;                                                 0000711804843011  
 END;                                                                   0000711904844011  
                                                                        0000712004845011  
$SET PAGE                                                               0000712104846011

 PROCEDURE LEVANTA ;                                                    0000712304848011  
%********************************************************************** 0000712404849011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000712504850011

 PROCEDURE ABRE_BASEDIA;                                                0000713304858011  
%---------------------------------------------------------------------- 0000713404859011  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000713504860011  
  S151BD10MOVDIA151.TITLE := BD_NAME;                                   0000713604861011  
  OPEN INQUIRY S151BD10MOVDIA151:DMERR;                                 0000713704862011  
  IF BOOLEAN(DMERR) THEN                                                0000713804863011  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000713904864011  
     ERRORDB            ;                                               0000714004865011  
     MYSELF.STATUS := -1 ;                                              0000714104866011  
  END                                                                   0000714204867011  
  ELSE                                                                  0000714304868011  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000714404869011  
     CAUSE(BASE_OPEN);                                                  0000714504870011  
     BASE_OK := TRUE;                                                   0000714604871011  
  END;                                                                  0000714704872011  
 END OF ABRE_BASEDIA;                                                   0000714804873011

%-------------------------------------------------------------          0000715404879011

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%CONTROLVERS;                                          0000715604881011  
 WHILE INT := LINKLIBRARY(SOPORTE) < 0 DO                               0000715704882011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000715804883011  
           % ERROR EN LIGA                                              0000715904884011  
       END;                                                             0000716004885011

                                                                        0000716204887011  
 WHILE INT := DELINKLIBRARY(SOPORTE) < 0 DO                             0000716304888011  
       BEGIN                                                            0000716404889011  
           % ERROR EN DESLIGA                                           0000716504890011  
       END;                                                             0000716604891011

 CSI_LIB := INTEGER(DATOS_HOST,2);                                      0000716804893011  
                                                                        0000716904894011  
 REPLACE NOM_LIB BY MYSELF.NAME;                                        0000717004895011  
 SIST_LIB := INTEGER(POINTER(NOM_LIB)+31,3);                            0000717104896011  
 CONTROLVERS;        %% ACCESA CONTROL DE VERSIONES                     0000717204897011  
 IF SIST_LIB = 404 OR SIST_LIB = 707 OR SIST_LIB = 804 THEN             0000717304897021  
    CLAVES_CFR                                                          0000717404897031  
 ELSE                                                                   0000717504897041  
    CARGACVES;          %% CARGA CLAVES DE CATALOGOS %%                 0000717604898011  
 CARGAFECHAS;                                                           0000717704899011  
 ABRE_BASEDIA;                                                          0000717804900011  
                                                                        0000717904901011  
 END LEVANTA;                                                           0000718004902011

                                                                        0000719404915011  
  RESET(BASE_OPEN);                                                     0000719504916011
